PDF 16 / UNIT 0 / heading
SOURCE: I = DEDICATION
CHINESE: 第1场  题献

PDF 16 / UNIT 1 / stage
SOURCE: You come, hovering forms, as in youth's day
You once appeared to my so muddled gaze.
Shall I now hold you fast, shall I essay
To turn again to that so-muddied daze?
You throng around me! Good! Rule as you may,
As from the mists you rise out of that haze;
The magic air about you stirs my breast
With youthful tumult and divine unrest.
You bring with you visions of happy days,
And many a dear loved shadow ascends to view;
Like some faint haunting old forgotten lays,
Love, first love, friendship come back with you;
My heart looks back at life’s bewildered maze,
And pains long laid to rest arise anew
And name those loved ones lost before their day,
Swept, while life was beautiful, away.
It saddens me to think they will not hear
These songs to whom my earliest songs I sang;
They are all gone, those gathered friends so dear,
Echoes hushed that once responsive rang;
My sorrows now fall only on the stranger’s ear,
Whose praises to my heart are a bitter pang,
And all who once heard my song, and flattered,
If still alive, around the world are scattered.
A yearning fills my soul, unknown so long,
To that sad spirit world again to go;
Like an Aeolian harp, my whispered song
Now trembles in the air in accents low;
A shudder thrills me as old memories throng,
The stern heart melts, tears fast on teardrops flow;
What I possess seems far, far off to me,
And what once was becomes reality.
CHINESE: 你们又来了，飘忽的形影，
如青春时曾现于我迷惘的眼前。
如今我能否将你们握紧，
是否还敢重返那混沌的旧年？
你们围拢过来！好吧，任你们引领，
从迷雾深处升起，从朦胧中显现；
你们周身的魔幻气息震动我的胸膛，
激起青春的骚动、神圣的不安。

你们带来幸福岁月的景象，
多少亲爱的身影又浮现在眼前；
如一支幽幽回荡、早已忘却的旧歌，
初恋与友情也随你们归还。
我的心回望那人生的迷宫，
久已平息的痛楚又重新涌现，
唤出那些过早离去的亲人，
生命尚且美好，他们却已被卷走。

想到他们再也听不见这些歌，
我便悲伤——我最初的歌曾向他们唱。
昔日相聚的挚友，如今都已离散，
曾经应答的回声，也早已沉寂。
我的哀愁，如今只有陌生人听见，
他们的赞美，却使我的心隐隐受伤。
当年听过我的歌、给过我赞许的人，
若还活着，也已散落在天涯各方。

一种久违的渴望充满我的灵魂，
想再往那寂寥的幽灵世界去；
仿佛风神的琴，我低低的歌声，
如今在空气里微微战栗。
旧日的记忆纷至沓来，我浑身一颤，
坚硬的心融化了，泪水接连涌出；
我所有的一切，仿佛已经远去，
而一度逝去的一切，却又成为现实。

PDF 17 / UNIT 2 / heading
SOURCE: II ~~ PRELUDE IN THE THEATER
CHINESE: 第2场  剧院序幕

PDF 17 / UNIT 3 / stage
SOURCE: The DIRECTOR.
The THEATER POET.
The JESTER.
CHINESE: 导演。戏剧诗人。小丑。

PDF 17 / UNIT 4 / speaker
SOURCE: DIRECTOR
CHINESE: 剧院经理

PDF 17 / UNIT 5 / verse
SOURCE: Very well now, friends, I want the truth.
It’s the least I can expect. After all,
this isn’t the first storm we've weathered together.
Tell me now,
what can we expect of this German tour?
I admit,
what I want most is to please the masses,
because as far as they're concerned
it’s live and let live.
The tent’s up, the floorboards laid,
and now it’s for us to show them a good time.
They're sitting out there now,
wide-eyed, eyebrows raised,
patiently waiting to be wonder struck.
I know what the public wants,
and I know how to give it to them,
but I’ve never been so embarrassed
as I am at this moment.
It’s true,
what they’re served up on our stages
is pretty awful stuff, but, by God,
they've devoured whole hordes of books.
So how do we concoct something fresh and new?
Perhaps even something important
that will also amuse them.
I admit, there’s nothing I like better
than a crowd bumping and jostling its way
in a mighty surge through our narrow gate to Paradise,
CHINESE: 好吧，朋友们，我要听真话。
这总是我起码能指望的。毕竟，
我们一起经历的风浪，早已不止一回。
告诉我，这次德国巡演，
究竟能有什么指望？
我承认，最要紧的是取悦大众，
因为在他们看来，
自己能过活，也该让别人过活。
帐篷搭起了，地板铺好了，
现在该我们让他们开心一场。
他们坐在外面，睁着眼，挑着眉，
耐心地等待着惊人的奇观。
我知道观众想要什么，
也知道怎样满足他们，
可我从没像此刻这样为难。
确实，我们舞台上端出的东西，
大半糟得很；可老天作证，
他们已经读下了成堆的书。
我们该怎样弄出点新鲜东西？
也许还得有些分量，
却又能让他们觉得有趣。
我承认，没有什么更让我快活，
胜过看见人群推推挤挤，
汹涌地穿过通向天堂的窄门，

PDF 18 / UNIT 6 / verse
SOURCE: up to the box office,
even before four, and like starving
men rushing the baker’s door,
all but breaking their necks to get a ticket.
Who but the poet can tame such a crowd
and work such a miracle?
And so, dear friend, I leave it to you!
CHINESE: 甚至在四点之前就到了售票处，
就像饥饿的人冲进面包店的门一样，
几乎是扭断脖子去买票。
除了诗人，还有谁能驯服这样的人群，
创造这样的奇迹呢？
所以，亲爱的朋友，
我把它留给你了！

PDF 18 / UNIT 7 / speaker
SOURCE: POEL
CHINESE: 诗人

PDF 18 / UNIT 8 / verse
SOURCE: Don't talk to me of that vulgar herd!
One sight offends the spirit!
Protect me from the mob
that drags us unwillingly into the abyss!
No, lead me to some peaceful retreat
where pure joy blossoms for the poet,
where Love and Friendship,
with God’s own hand,
create and nurture the poet's inspiration.
Ah!
The blessing that makes its way from deep inside us,
syllables that lips have stammered timidly into scenes
CHINESE: 别跟我谈论那群粗俗的人！
一看就让人心生不快！
保护我免受那些不情愿地将我们拖入深渊的暴徒的伤害！
不，带我去一些宁静的隐居处，
在那里，诗人纯粹的快乐绽放，
爱和友谊，用上帝的双手，
创造和培育诗人的灵感。
啊!从我们内心深处发出的祝福，
嘴唇怯怯地结结巴巴的音节进入场景

PDF 18 / UNIT 9 / stage
SOURCE: (successful now, another time not)
CHINESE: （这次成功了，下次不成功）

PDF 18 / UNIT 10 / verse
SOURCE: are swallowed in a single violent moment.
Only years will tell, years of ripening,
till it blossoms into perfection.
What glitters and flashes lives for a day.
The true is never lost to posterity.
CHINESE: 在一个暴力的瞬间被吞噬。
只有岁月才能证明一切，
岁月的成熟，直到它绽放出完美的花朵。
闪闪发光的东西只存在一天。
真实的东西永远不会被后人遗忘。

PDF 18 / UNIT 11 / speaker
SOURCE: JESTER
CHINESE: 丑角

PDF 18 / UNIT 12 / verse
SOURCE: Posterity!
Spare me the word! I hate it, hate it!
Laughter is what we need.
Here! Now! Fun!
It’s what they've come for.
Let's give it to them.
One stout and sturdy clown who knows his business
is nothing to be sneezed at.
CHINESE: 后世！
饶了我吧！
我讨厌它，讨厌它！
笑声正是我们所需要的。
这里！
现在！
乐趣！
这就是他们来这里的目的。
让我们把它交给他们吧。
一个粗壮、健壮、知道自己的事的小丑是不容小觑的。

PDF 19 / UNIT 13 / verse
SOURCE: He knows what it takes to please the masses,
and the bigger the crowd
the easier it is to work them.
So much for that.
Now, show us your best, my boy.
Let imagination sing with all its choirs—
reason, common sense, feeling and passion, too.
Just don’t forget to throw in a little folly.
CHINESE: 他知道怎样才能取悦大众，
而受众越多，就越容易吸引他们。
就这么多了。
现在，向我们展示你最好的一面吧，
我的孩子。
让想象力与它的所有合唱团一起歌唱——理性、常识、感觉和激情。
只是不要忘记加入一些愚蠢的东西。

PDF 19 / UNIT 14 / speaker
SOURCE: DIRECTOR
CHINESE: 剧院经理

PDF 19 / UNIT 15 / verse
SOURCE: Not to mention plenty of action.
It’s what they've come for,
to see,
to see things happen,
its what they like.
Unreel a string of actions before their eyes,
make them gape in wonder,
and you've got them where you want them,
in the palm of your hand.
Mass is what it takes to please the masses,
mass and variety.
In the end,
everyone chooses what he most likes,
and goes home happy.
But whatever work you manage to put on,
give it in pieces,
serve it up like a ragout,
and you'll have a success.
It’s easy,
as soon as it simmers you dish it out.
Why give it to them whole
when they'll pick it apart in any case?
CHINESE: 更不用说大量的行动了。
这就是他们来这里的目的，
就是为了看到、看到事情发生，
这是他们喜欢的。
在他们眼前展开一连串的动作，
让他们目瞪口呆，
然后你就可以将他们带到你想要的地方，
就在你的手掌上。
大众是取悦大众、大众和多样性所需要的。
最后，每个人都选择了自己最喜欢的，
高高兴兴地回家了。
但无论你做了什么工作，
把它分成几部分，像炖肉一样端上来，
你就会成功。
很简单，煮开后就可以出锅了。
既然他们无论如何都会把它拆开，
为什么要把它整个给他们呢？

CHINESE: 诗人

PDF 19 / UNIT 17 / verse
SOURCE: How can you?
How can you ask me this?
I’m an artist,
CHINESE: 你怎么能？
你怎么能问我这个？
我是一名艺术家，

PDF 20 / UNIT 18 / verse
SOURCE: I don't stoop to such things.
Is this what you've come down to?
Lowered your standards to the level of hackwork?
A butcher’s trade?
CHINESE: 我不会屈服于这样的事情。
这就是你的落脚点吗？
把你的标准降低到黑客工作的水平？
屠夫生意？

PDF 20 / UNIT 19 / speaker
SOURCE: DIRECTOR
CHINESE: 剧院经理

PDF 20 / UNIT 20 / verse
SOURCE: Complain all you like,
it makes no difference.
What matters most in doing a job right
is having the proper tools.
Know the people you're writing for:
it’s soft wood you have to split, not hard.
One of them comes bored out of his wits,
another with a belly about to burst,
and worst of all,
many come straight from reading the newspapers.
They come to us distracted,
absentmindedly,
as though they were off to a masquerade,
only curiosity leading them on.
And the ladies,
ah, the ladies, they appear
decked out in all their finery,
mounting their own little play,
and what’s more, without pay.
What are you dreaming up there in your ivory tower?
Why should a full house make you happy?
Take a closer look at your customers.
Half are cold and the other half coarse.
One looks forward after the show
to a game of cards, another
to a wild romp with a whore.
Is it for that you torture the Muses?
Ah, poor fools!
My advice is pile it high as it will go,
CHINESE: 你想抱怨什么就抱怨什么，
没有什么区别。
 正确完成工作最重要的是拥有合适的工具。
了解你正在为谁写作：
你必须劈开的是软木，
而不是硬木。
其中一个人感到无聊得快要疯了，
另一个人则肚子快要撑破了，
最糟糕的是，许多人是直接读完报纸而来的。
 他们心不在焉、心不在焉地来到我们身边，
就像是去参加一场化装舞会，
只有好奇心引导着他们。
而女士们，啊，女士们，
她们打扮得漂漂亮亮，
上演着自己的小戏，
更重要的是，没有报酬。
你在象牙塔里梦想着什么？
为什么满屋子会让你开心？
仔细观察您的客户。
一半是冷的，另一半是粗糙的。
演出结束后，有人期待一场纸牌游戏，
有人期待与妓女的疯狂嬉闹。
你就是为了折磨缪斯女神吗？
啊，可怜的傻瓜们！
我的建议是尽可能把它堆高，

PDF 21 / UNIT 21 / verse
SOURCE: and then a little higher.
Confuse them with mystification is best;
it’s easier than trying to satisfy them.
But what's got you now?
Are you in pain or rapture?
CHINESE: 然后再高一点。
最好用神秘化的方式来混淆它们；
这比试图满足他们更容易。
但你现在怎么了？
你是痛苦还是狂喜？

PDF 21 / UNIT 22 / speaker
SOURCE: POEM
CHINESE: 诗人

PDF 21 / UNIT 23 / verse
SOURCE: Go on, go, go find yourself another slave!
Do you know what you're asking?
You expect a poet frivolously to squander
the greatest gift that Nature offers him!
The right to be a man!
For you!
No!
What is it allows him to stir men’s hearts?
What is it allows him to conquer Nature’s elements?
One thing only:
the harmony that flows from the poet's breast,
that gathers in its embrace the multiplicity
of Nature’s discrepancies,
and draws them back into his heart
as a harmonious whole.
When Nature winds life’s endless thread
indifferently on her spindle, and all
life’s fractured sounds roar in discord,
who harmonizes the monotonous, meaningless drone
and makes it sing in orderly choir?
Who invites the tempest to rage in passion?
And the sunset to glow in the earnest soul?
Who scatters springtime’s sweetest blossoms
on paths for young lovers to walk on?
Who twines from insignificant leaves
crowns to honor the brows of the deserving?
Who assures us of Olympus and unites the gods?
The power of Man made manifest in the poet.
CHINESE: 走吧，走吧，去找另一个奴隶吧！
你知道你在问什么吗？
你以为诗人会轻率地挥霍大自然赐予他的最伟大的礼物！
做人的权利！
为你！
不！
是什么让他能够撩动人心？
是什么让他能够征服自然元素？
只有一件事：从诗人胸中流淌出来的和谐，
将大自然的多样性差异聚集在怀抱中，
并将它们作为一个和谐的整体拉回到他的心中。
当大自然冷漠地将生命无尽的丝线缠绕在她的纺锤上，
所有生命断裂的声音都在不和谐地咆哮时，
是谁将单调无意义的嗡嗡声调和起来，
让它有序地唱诗班？
是谁在激情中邀请暴风雨肆虐？
夕阳照耀着真诚的灵魂？
谁将春天最甜美的花朵撒在小路上，
供年轻情侣行走？
谁用微不足道的叶冠缠绕来尊敬应得的眉毛？
谁给我们保证奥林匹斯山并团结众神？
人的力量在诗人身上得到了体现。

PDF 22 / UNIT 24 / speaker
SOURCE: JES CER
CHINESE: 丑角

PDF 22 / UNIT 25 / verse
SOURCE: All right, then, do it.
Use these marvelous powers of yours.
Conduct this business of forging verses
the way you'd manage a love affair.
You meet by chance,
feel a tingle,
stick around,
and bit by bit you get involved.
Happiness thrives,
then it’s endangered.
First you're enraptured,
then writhing in anguish.
And before you know it,
you've got a whole novel.
And that’s how it’s done.
So let’s do it.
Let’s give the play that way!
Plunge your hand into the fullness of life.
We all of us live it,
few of us know it;
but it’s interesting wherever you grab it.
Variety, color, confusion, error,
and then a tantalizing soupcon of truth.
That’s how the best beers are brewed,
a brew that refreshes and makes you think, too.
Ah, the best of youth will flock to your play,
eager for any revelation.
The sentimental will suck melancholy strength,
and this and that and everyone
will take from it what each has felt in his heart.
Being young they're as ready to weep as to laugh;
they still have ideals and delight in illusion.
Adults you can never please,
they've stopped growing,
but youth will always be grateful for what you offer.
CHINESE: 好吧，那就去做吧。
使用你的这些奇妙的力量。
以处理爱情的方式来处理锻造业务。
你们偶然相遇，感到刺痛，
坚持下去，一点一点地参与其中。
幸福会蓬勃发展，然后就会受到威胁。
首先你欣喜若狂，然后痛苦地翻滚。
在你不知不觉中，你已经有了一本完整的小说。
事情就是这样完成的。
那么让我们开始吧。
我们就这样演戏吧！
将你的手投入到充实的生活中。
我们所有人都生活在其中，
但很少有人知道；
但无论你在哪里抓住它，
它都会很有趣。
多样性、色彩、混乱、错误，
然后是诱人的真理汤。
这就是最好的啤酒的酿造方法，
这种啤酒既能提神，
又能引发思考。
啊，最优秀的年轻人会涌向你的表演，
渴望得到任何启示。
多愁善感会吸收忧郁的力量，
而这个、那个、每个人都会从中汲取每个人内心的感受。
年轻的时候，他们既想哭，
又想笑；
他们仍然有理想，乐于幻想。
你永远无法取悦成年人，
他们已经停止成长，
但年轻人永远会感激你所提供的一切。

PDF 23 / UNIT 26 / speaker
SOURCE: POET
CHINESE: 诗人

PDF 23 / UNIT 27 / verse
SOURCE: Then give me back those days again
when I myself was still unfinished,
when an endless fountain of songs
welled from my soul, when the world to me
was veiled in mystery, when each bud
was a miracle of promise
as I went out to pick the thousand flowers
that thronged the valley.
I had nothing then,
but it was enough—
the yearning for truth and the joy of illusion.
Give back to me each reckless longing,
the deep and anguished happiness,
the strength of hate,
the power of love!
Oh, give me back my youth again!
CHINESE: 然后让我再次回到那些日子，
那时我自己还没有完成，
当无尽的歌曲喷泉从我的灵魂中涌出时，
当我的世界被神秘的面纱笼罩时，
当我出去采摘山谷里的一千朵鲜花时，
每个花蕾都是一个承诺的奇迹。
那时我一无所有，
但已经足够了——对真理的向往和幻想的快乐。
还给我每一个不计后果的渴望，
深深而痛苦的幸福，
恨的力量，爱的力量！
哦，让我再次青春吧！

PDF 23 / UNIT 28 / speaker
SOURCE: JESTER
CHINESE: 丑角

PDF 23 / UNIT 29 / verse
SOURCE: Youth, old friend, is what you'll need
when enemies threaten in the heat of battle,
when pretty girls throw their arms around you
and squeeze for all they're worth,
when in the distance the victory wreath beckons
from the hard-won goal, when after the whirl
of the frenzied dance you drink away the night.
But to strike the familiar strings boldly yet gracefully,
to wander leisurely life’s sweet digressions
to a self-set goal, that, old friend,
is your duty,
and we honor you no less for it.
Age doesn’t make us childish, as they say,
it only finds the child that’s with us still.
CHINESE: 老朋友，当敌人在激烈的战斗中威胁时，
当漂亮的女孩张开双臂拥抱你时，
当远处胜利的花环在来之不易的目标上招手时，
当你在疯狂的舞蹈之后喝掉夜晚时，
老朋友，你需要的是青春。
但是，大胆而优雅地弹奏熟悉的琴弦，
在悠闲的生活中漫步，
走向自我设定的目标，
这是你的责任，老朋友，
我们也因此向你致敬。
正如人们所说，年龄不会让我们变得幼稚，
它只会发现仍然和我们在一起的孩子。

PDF 23 / UNIT 31 / verse
SOURCE: Enough talk!
Let’s get down to business.
Action!
While you two trade compliments,
CHINESE: 够了！

行动！
当你们两个互相恭维时，

PDF 24 / UNIT 32 / verse
SOURCE: you could be doing something useful.
And don’t complain you're not in the mood.
Wait for it and it never arrives.
And you, you call yourself a poet?
Then let’s see some poetry!
What we want here is strong drink,
so let’s get it brewing.
What you put off today is put off tomorrow,
and we haven't a day to lose.
What we need here is a man of resolution,
one who seizes the Possible by the forelock
and wont let go, a man who refuses
to stop because he’s committed.
You know what the German theater is like,
we can experiment all we please.
So don’t spare on scenery and stage effects.
Stars and suns and moons are had for the asking.
There’s fire, and water enough for a flood,
and plenty of cliffs to jump off of,
not to mention an assortment of birds and beasts.
Show us on this petty stage of ours
the full scope of creation,
and travel with the speed of imagination
the course from Heaven through the world
to the bowels of Hell!
CHINESE: 你可能正在做一些有用的事情。
不要抱怨你没有心情。
等待它，它永远不会到来。
而你，你称自己为诗人？
那我们就来看看诗吧！
我们想要的是烈性饮料，
所以我们来冲泡吧。
今天推迟的事情明天也会推迟，
我们没有一天可以浪费。
我们在这里需要的是一个有决心的人，
一个抓住可能性并且不放手的人，
一个因为承诺而拒绝停止的人。
你知道德国剧院是什么样的，
我们可以随心所欲地进行实验。
所以在布景和舞台效果上也不吝惜。
星星、太阳和月亮是有求必应的。
这里有火，有足以引发洪水的水，
还有大量可供跳下的悬崖，
更不用说各种各样的鸟类和野兽了。
在我们这个小小的舞台上向我们展示创造的全部范围，
并以想象的速度从天堂穿越世界到达地狱的深处！

PDF 26 / UNIT 46 / verse
SOURCE: Lord, since you approach us once again,
and ask how we're getting on;
and since in the past
I’ve never been made feel unwelcome,
I present myself once more among your servants.
Youll forgive me, I know,
if | make no pretty speeches,
but, you see, I’m just not very good at it,
not even if I’m scoffed at and scorned.
I'd make you laugh, I know, with my sorry pathos—
except that you gave up laughter eons ago.
I mean, really,
what can I say about your sun and your worlds?
Nothing; nothing at all.
All I see is how men torment themselves.
And as for that little lord of creation,
he’s no less “wondrous” than on the day you made him.
And yet, I'd say, he might be better off
if you hadn't blessed him with that glimpse of heavenly light
that he calls Reason, and which he uses
to make himself more beastly than any beast.
You'll pardon my saying so,
but to me he resembles more the long-legged cricket
that does nothing but leap about,
higher and higher,
only to end up back where he began,
deep in the grass singing the same old song.
If only he'd stay in the grass!
But he pokes his nose into every kind of filth.
CHINESE: 主啊，既然你又来到我们中间，
问我们日子过得怎样；
况且从前我到这里，
也不曾受到冷眼，
所以我又来，站在你众仆之中。
我想，你会原谅我说不出漂亮话，
因为我实在不擅长这套，
任人讥笑、任人轻蔑，也学不会。
要是我学着慷慨激昂，只怕会让你发笑——
可惜你早在千万年前，就不再笑了。
说真的，你的太阳、你的世界，
我能谈些什么呢？
什么也谈不了，一点也谈不了。
我只看见人怎样折磨自己。
至于那小小的万物之灵，
从你造出他的那天起，就一直那么“奇妙”。
不过依我看，他也许还能过得好些，
假如你不赐给他那一线天光。
他把它叫作理性，却拿它来使自己
比任何野兽都更加兽性十足。
请恕我直言，在我看来，
他更像一只长腿蟋蟀，
只顾着蹦来跳去，越蹦越高，
最后还是回到原处，
钻在草里，唱那首老调。
要是他老实待在草里，也就罢了！
可他偏要把鼻子伸进每一种污秽里。

CHINESE: 我站在这里！

PDF 30 / UNIT 83 / verse
SOURCE: Not one jot wiser than before.
Oh, I have my degrees,
they call me Master of Arts, and Doctor!
And for these ten years now
I’ve led my students around by the nose,
upstairs, downstairs, backwards, forwards—
and learned?
That there’s nothing to be learned.
Nothing!
And that is the dread that eats at my heart’s core.
Oh, but I’m no dunce.
I’m better than all these “doctors,” these “masters,”
these “clerks” and “priests,”
and not even fear of Hell or the Devil
can make me tremble with scruples or doubts.
And what is my reward for all that?
The loss of all joy in life.
Why pretend?
Why assume I know anything worth knowing?
Why delude myself I can teach anything
to improve or convert mankind?
And what has it got me?
Just this!
I’m a pauper.
Or nearly so.
I have neither property nor wealth,
nor worldly honor.
No glory.
Show me a dog who would lead such a life!
CHINESE: 比起从前，半点也没有更聪明。
哦，我倒是有学位，
人们称我为硕士，称我为博士！
整整十年了，
我牵着学生的鼻子，上楼下楼，
向前向后，兜来转去——
究竟学到了什么？
就是：我们什么也无法真正知道。
什么也不能！
这恐惧啃噬着我的心。
不过，我可不是笨蛋，
我比那些“博士”、那些“硕士”，
那些“文士”和“教士”强得多；
连地狱与魔鬼的恐吓，
都不能叫我因顾忌和疑惧而战栗。
然而这一切给了我什么回报？
生活的乐趣，全都失去了。
何必装模作样？
凭什么认定自己懂得值得懂的东西？
何必骗自己还能教给人什么，

我究竟得到了什么？
就是这样！
我一贫如洗，

我既无产业，也无财富，
更没有世间的荣誉，
没有声名。
哪条狗肯过这样的日子！

PDF 31 / UNIT 84 / verse
SOURCE: And that’s why I’ve turned my mind to magic,
to see if the spirit’s power
can help me wrest secrets from the spirit world and
save me the dreary sour sweat of saying

at its innermost core,
and seeing its working and germinal forces,
end this endless traffic in words.
Ah, full moon!

You that I have watched at my desk
as l’ve sat here awake.
You that I have seen climbing the sky
through many a long night,
shining across my books and papers!
Melancholy friend!

in the high mountains,
bathe in your beloved radiance,

Scull!
Stuck in this God-damned hole in the wall,

confined by piles, by heaps, of ancient books,

CHINESE: 所以我才转而求助魔法，

啊！
可我还在这里！
仍在这里！

连上天蒙福的光，

熏黑的纸页，堆到了天花板！

PDF 32 / UNIT 85 / verse
SOURCE: retorts and canisters thrown helter-skelter,

CHINESE: 蒸馏瓶和罐子乱七八糟地乱扔，

出去！

大自然会教你灵魂如何与灵魂对话。
对魔法符号进行枯燥的猜测是多么浪费啊！
灵魂，我感觉到你在附近徘徊。
如果你听到的话请回答我！

PDF 32 / UNIT 86 / stage
SOURCE: (He opens the book and perceives the sign of the Macrocosm.)
CHINESE: （他打开书，感知到宏观宇宙的迹象。）

PDF 32 / UNIT 87 / verse
SOURCE: Ah!
CHINESE: 啊！

PDF 33 / UNIT 88 / verse
SOURCE: What is this vision that suddenly seizes my senses!
I feel the holy fire of youth
coursing through every vein and nerve!
What god was it wrote these symbols
that calm the storm of torment inside me;
that fill my wretched heart with joy;
that with some mysterious force
reveals the forces of Nature around me?
Am | a god?
I?
Oh, suddenly it’s all so clear, so clear!
I see in the pure lines of this symbol
all of Creative Nature at work.
I understand the philosopher for the first time.
“The spirit world is open, not barred.
Your mind is closed, your heart is dead!
Rise up, young man, and bathe your breast
in the rosy flush of dawn!”
CHINESE: 什么景象，忽然攫住我的感官！
我感到青春的神圣火焰，
奔流过每一条血管、每一根神经！
是哪一位神写下这些符号，
平息我内心苦痛的风暴，
使我这可怜的心充满喜悦，
又以某种神秘的力量，
揭开周遭自然的种种力量？
我是神吗？
我？
啊，忽然全都清楚了，如此清楚！
我在这符号纯净的线条里，
看见整个创造的自然在活动。
我第一次明白了哲人的话：
“精灵的世界是敞开的，并未封锁；
封闭的是你的心智，死去的是你的心！
起来吧，年轻人，让你的胸膛，
沐浴在破晓玫瑰色的霞光中！”

CHINESE: （他凝视着这个符号。）

PDF 33 / UNIT 90 / verse

Ah!

CHINESE: 啊！

啊!但只是一场表演。

CHINESE: 无限的自然，我该从哪里把握你？

CHINESE: 这个符号对我的影响是多么不同啊！

PDF 40 / UNIT 147 / stage

PDF 40 / UNIT 148 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 40 / UNIT 149 / verse
SOURCE: How is it that they never give up hope?
Here is a man who clings for his very life
to everything that is shallow, digging greedily
in the earth for treasures, and rejoices
when he finds an earthworm in the earth!
What right has a human voice to speak here?
And especially the voice of such a dreary pedant?
Here where the air around me teemed with spirits!
Sull, I thank you this once,
you sorriest of all earth’s creatures.
You wrenched me from a despair
that was close to destroying me.
An apparition so vast it dwarfed me!
A nothing!
I, image of the Godhead!
I, so certain I would look into the mirror
of eternal truth! Already basking
in the pristine light of heaven’s glory,
trailing mortality behind me!
What a fool!
Thinking myself greater than angelic cherubim,
able unhindered to flow through Nature’s veins
and revel in the act of creation like the gods!
CHINESE: 他们为何不放弃希望？
这是一个人，一生都执着于一切浅薄的事物，
贪婪地在地里挖掘宝藏，
当他在地里发现一条蚯蚓时，
他会感到高兴！
人类有什么权利在这里说话？
尤其是这样一个沉闷的学究的声音？
在这里，我周围的空气充满了灵气！
苏尔，我再次感谢你，
你是地球上最悲伤的生物。
你把我从近乎毁灭的绝望中拉了出来。
一个巨大的幽灵让我相形见绌！
啥也没有啊！
我，神格影像！
我，如此确信我会照照永恒真理的镜子！
已经沐浴在天堂荣耀的原始光芒中，
将死亡抛在身后！
真是个傻瓜！
认为自己比天使般的小天使更伟大，
能够不受阻碍地流经大自然的血管，
像众神一样陶醉于创造的行为！

PDF 41 / UNIT 150 / verse
SOURCE: Ah, but the punishment!
One thundering word has brought me to my knees.
Equal to you?
No.
The power to summon you here?
Yes:
Yes, that I had.
But not the power to hold you.
O that moment!
That supreme moment
when at once I felt so great and so small!
And then to be thrust back, ruthlessly, into uncertainty,
the fated lot of man.
Who will teach me?
What must I shun?
Do I deny my urge,
my quest to seek the source of all life?
Ah, not only the deeds we do,
but those done fo us,
turn us from our goal.
Our mind’s most glorious thoughts are
forever violated by the vulgar dross of matter.
Once we achieve the good of this world,
ideals are despised and deemed illusion,
and only comfort serves.
Noble sentiments that once were life’s breath
are drowned in earthly chaos.
There was a time when the winged and hopeful flight
of imagination soared out into infinity;
but now, when misfortunes come,
one after another,
a tiny space is all it needs.
CHINESE: 啊，但是惩罚！
一声雷鸣般的话语让我跪了下来。
和你一样吗？
不，有能力召唤你来这里？
是的：是的，我有过。
但没有力量来束缚你。
噢，那一刻！
那个至高无上的时刻，
我立刻感到如此伟大又如此渺小！
然后被无情地推回不确定的境地，
这就是人类的命运。
谁来教我？
我必须避免什么？
我是否否认我对寻找所有生命之源的渴望和追求？
啊，不仅我们所做的事，
而且那些为我们所做的事，
都使我们偏离了我们的目标。
我们心灵最辉煌的思想永远被庸俗的物质渣滓所侵犯。
一旦我们实现了这个世界的美好，
理想就会被鄙视，被视为幻想，
只有舒适才能发挥作用。
曾经是生命气息的高贵情感被尘世的混沌淹没了。
曾经有一段时间，想象的翅膀和希望飞翔到无限。
但现在，当不幸接连发生时，
它所需要的只是一个小小的空间。

PDF 42 / UNIT 151 / verse
SOURCE: Care nestles itself in the heart’s depths,
where it brings unease and mysterious pains,
destroying all life’s joy,
always hiding behind new disguises,
unknown masks.
She may come as house and home,
as wife and child, as fire, water,
dagger and poison, making you quake
at the thought of what never happens,
and weep over things you never lose.
I’m not like the gods.
How well I know that.
I’m a worm in the dust crushed by a passing foot.
And what is this but dust?
This prison ’m in with its hundred compartments?
Rubbish!
All of it!
Trifles!
Nothing!
A moth-eaten world!
Is this where I’m to find happiness?
And after a thousand books discover that men
have tormented themselves eternally
and only a few have found happiness?
CHINESE: 忧愁潜伏在内心深处，
带来不安和神秘的痛苦，
摧毁一切生活的欢乐，
总是隐藏在新的伪装、未知的面具后面。
她可能会以房屋和家的形式出现，
以妻子和孩子的形式出现，
以火、水、匕首和毒药的形式出现，
让你因想到从未发生过的事情而颤抖，
为你从未失去的东西而哭泣。
我不像诸神。
我多么清楚这一点。
我是尘土中的一条虫子，
被路过的脚踩碎。
这除了尘埃之外又是什么？
这所监狱有一百个隔间？
垃圾！
所有这一切！
琐事！
没有什么！
一个被虫蛀的世界！
这就是我寻找幸福的地方吗？
当一千本书发现人们永远折磨自己而只有少数人找到幸福之后？

PDF 42 / UNIT 152 / stage
SOURCE: (He looks at a skull.)
CHINESE: （他看着一个头骨。）

PDF 42 / UNIT 153 / verse
SOURCE: What are you looking at?
You grin, and your skull is hollow, and you
whisper to me that, just like me, you once groped
blindly for enlightenment and truth,
and, like me, went hopelessly astray.
Even my instruments mock me.
Wheels and cogs, cylinders and bridles.
CHINESE: 你在看什么？
你咧着嘴笑，你的头骨是空的，
你低声对我说，你和我一样，
曾经盲目地摸索着启蒙和真理，
但和我一样，绝望地误入歧途。
连我的乐器都在嘲笑我。
轮子和齿轮、圆柱体和辔头。

PDF 43 / UNIT 154 / verse
SOURCE: Useless!
What do you open?
You were my keys.
I stood at the gates panting with expectation,
but you unlocked nothing.
Even in the sun’s uncompromising light
Nature remains a mystery.
Demure, chaste, she lifts no veil.
Nothing.
And you, Faustus, with your levers and screws,
are impotent in the face of Nature;
as impotent as these ancient instruments
I’ve never used:
my father’s tools, grimy,
gathering soot from my smoking lamp.
What a fool.
I should have squandered the little I had,
rather than waste my life under the weight of this!
To own what the past has left, you must use it.
Not to is a terrible burden.
Only what the moment creates can serve the moment.
CHINESE: 没用！
你打开什么？
你是我的钥匙。
我站在门口气喘吁吁地期待着，
但你什么也没打开。
即使在阳光下，大自然仍然是一个谜。
她端庄、贞洁，不摘面纱。
没有什么。
而你，浮士德，你的杠杆和螺丝，
在大自然面前是无能为力的；
就像这些我从未使用过的古老工具一样无能：
我父亲的工具，肮脏不堪，
积聚着我冒烟的灯里的烟灰。
真是个傻瓜。
我应该挥霍我所拥有的一点点，
而不是在这样的重压下浪费我的生命！
要拥有过去留下的东西，
你必须使用它。
不这样做是一个可怕的负担。
只有当下创造的东西才能为当下服务。

PDF 43 / UNIT 155 / stage
SOURCE: (He sees a flask on a shelf.)
CHINESE: （他看到架子上有一个烧瓶。）

PDF 43 / UNIT 156 / verse
SOURCE: That flask—
why does it draw my eye like a magnet?
It clears my mind like moonlight
suddenly flooding a dark forest.
Come,
come down, you rarest, you holiest of vials!
I praise the human cunning that made you,
you distillation of sleep-inducing herbs,
quintessence of subtle poisons!
CHINESE: 那个烧瓶——为什么它像磁铁一样吸引着我的目光？
它让我的思绪变得清晰，
就像月光突然淹没了黑暗的森林。
来吧，下来吧，你这个最稀有、最神圣的瓶子！
我赞扬人类的狡猾，
正是它们使你——催眠草药的精华——微妙毒药的精髓！

PDF 44 / UNIT 157 / verse
SOURCE: Show your master how you can save him from this.
I look at you and my pain is eased.
I grasp you and my striving lessens.
The flood tide of my spirit slowly ebbs,
and I am carried out onto the open sea
where the great waters glisten below me
and a new day lures me to new shores.
A fiery chariot sweeps down for me!
I’m ready now for the journey,
a new course, new spheres of pure activity!
How exalted!
The divine joy of it!
And yet, just now you were a miserable worm.
How do you dare to deserve this, Faust?
I'll turn my back to the sweet light of the sun,
tear open the gates that
frightened men pass by in silence!
The time has come to prove by deeds that man
will not be intimidated by the gods,
but be their equal, bold enough to face
that dark cavern where imagination condemns itself
to torment, force his way into the narrow,
fire-ringed passage of Hell,
and to do it serenely,
even if it ends in annihilation!
Come down to me now, long-forgotten chalice,
out of your ancient case.
My mind floods with memories of family gatherings and
guests who did their duty in making toasts,
amusing us all with rhymes
to explain your artful decorations,
and then drain the bowl at a single draught.
CHINESE: 向你的主人展示你如何才能把他从困境中拯救出来。
我看着你，我的痛苦就减轻了。
我抓住了你，我的努力就减弱了。
我精神上的潮水慢慢退去，
我被带到了大海上，
波涛汹涌的海水在我脚下闪闪发光，
新的一天引诱我到新的海岸。
一辆烈火战车为我席卷而下！
我现在已经准备好踏上旅程，
新的路线，新的纯粹活动领域！
多么崇高啊！
这是神圣的喜悦！
然而，刚才你还是一条可怜的虫子。
浮士德，你怎么敢受这样的待遇？
我将背对甜蜜的阳光，
撕开那些受惊的人们默默经过的大门！
是时候用行动来证明，
人类不会被诸神吓倒，
而是与众神平等，
勇敢地面对那个想象力注定要受折磨的黑暗洞穴，
强行进入狭窄的、被火环包围的地狱通道，
并且平静地做到这一点，
即使最终会导致毁灭！
现在，从你古老的箱子里拿出久违的圣杯，
到我这里来吧。
我的脑海里充满了家庭聚会和客人们的回忆，
他们尽职尽责地祝酒，
用押韵来解释你巧妙的装饰，
让我们大家都开心，
然后一下子就把碗喝干了。

PDF 45 / UNIT 158 / verse
SOURCE: I have no friend of my youth to pass it on to,

I pledge it now, with all my heart,
to the new day.
CHINESE: 我没有年轻时的朋友可以将它传递给我，

这是一种很快就会让人陶醉的饮料，
是我自己准备的饮料，
现在将其黑色液体倒入你满溢的杯子中。
我现在全心全意地向新的一天保证。

PDF 46 / UNIT 169 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 46 / UNIT 170 / verse
SOURCE: Heavenly tones, so gentle, so strong,
why do you seek me here in the dust?
Send your sounds to weaker men.
I hear your message,
but lack the faith that fathers the miraculous.
I don’t aspire to those spheres
that peal out your message of salvation.
I wouldn't dare.
And yet,
I've known these sounds from my earliest youth,
and even now they call me back to life.
Time was when in the stillness of the Sabbath
the kiss of heavenly love descended upon me.
Pealing bells then were full of promise,
and prayer a rapturous delight.
An unknown sweet yearning
would lure me to rove the woods and fields
where floods of tears gave birth to a new world.
This is the hymn that announced the games of childhood
and the untroubled joy of springtime celebrations.
It is this memory that holds me back from
taking this last, this solemn step.
Sweet and heavenly songs, ring out!
My tears well up and earth wins me back again.
CHINESE: 天籁般的音调，那么温柔，
那么强烈，你为何到尘埃里来找我？
将你的声音发送给较弱的人。
我听到了你的信息，
但缺乏创造奇迹的信念。
我并不渴望那些能够传达你的救赎信息的领域。
我不敢。
然而，我从很小的时候起就听过这些声音，
即使现在它们也让我起死回生。
在安息日的寂静中，
天国之爱的吻降临在我身上。
钟声敲响充满了希望，
祈祷也充满了欣喜若狂。
一种未知的甜蜜的向往，
引诱我去漫游树林和田野，
那里的泪水催生了一个新的世界。
这首赞美诗宣告了童年的游戏和春天庆祝活动的无忧无虑的欢乐。
正是这段记忆阻止了我迈出这最后、这庄严的一步。
甜美天籁之歌，响起！
我的眼泪夺眶而出，
大地又把我赢了回来。

maybe in Turkey,
they're pounding each others’ heads together.

and watch bright ships sail downstream,
and then at twilight wend your way back home
and thank God for the blessings of peace.
CHINESE: 在周日和节假日，

也许是在土耳其，他们在一起互相敲打着对方的头。
你站在窗前，喝干酒杯，

然后在黄昏时分踏上回家的路，

PDF 56 / UNIT 258 / speaker

CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 56 / UNIT 259 / verse
SOURCE: A few more steps to that rock and we'll rest.
—How often I’ve sat here alone in thought,
tormenting myself with prayer and fasting.
Rich in hope, firm in faith,
with tears and sighs and wringing of hands
I tried to extort from the Lord an end to that plague.
The crowd’s applause sounds to me now like mockery.
If only you saw in my innermost soul
how little that father and son deserve such fame!
My father was an honorable man groping in darkness.
In all good faith, he brooded on Nature
and her sacred spheres,
but always in his odd capricious way—
never without honesty,
never without sincerity of heart—
but with tedious and fantastic means,
and that was the problem.
In the company of other adepts,
he locked himself in the black kitchen of necromancy,
where, with interminable recipes,
he mixed the mutually incompatible.
Patients died,
and no one bothered to count the survivors.
And so, armed with these hellish powders,
we raged through the region,
these mountains, these valleys,
more devastating than even the plague!
I myself gave the poison to thousands
who withered away; and now I must live to hear
the murderers praised to the skies.
CHINESE: 再走几步就到那块岩石了，
我们就休息了。
 ——我有多少次独自坐在这里沉思，
用祈祷和禁食来折磨自己。
我怀着丰富的希望，
坚定的信念，带着泪水、叹息和绞痛的双手，
试图向主祈求结束这场瘟疫。
人群的掌声现在对我来说就像是嘲笑。
要是你能在我内心深处看到那对父子是多么不配得到这样的名声就好了！
我的父亲是一位在黑暗中摸索的可敬的人。
他满怀善意地沉思于自然和她的神圣领域，
但总是以他奇怪的反复无常的方式——从来没有没有诚实，
从来没有没有真诚的心——而是用乏味而荒诞的手段，
这就是问题所在。
在其他行家的陪伴下，
他将自己锁在死灵术的黑色厨房里，
在那里，他用没完没了的食谱，
混合了互不相容的东西。
病人死了，没有人费心去统计幸存者。

我们肆虐了这个地区，
这些山脉，这些山谷，
其破坏性甚至比瘟疫还要严重！
我亲自把毒药给了成千上万的枯萎者；
现在我必须活着听到凶手被捧上天。

PDF 57 / UNIT 264 / verse
SOURCE: That man is happy who has any hope
of rising out of this sea of confusion!
What we need most is just what we don’t have,
and what we have is of no use whatever.
Ah,

with such melancholy thoughts?
Look there! Do you see?
Cottages nestled in their beds of greenery,
shimmering in the rays of the setting sun.
The sun retreats, moves on, and day is done,
scurrying off to nourish new life elsewhere.
Oh, how I wish for wings to lift me from earth and
strive forever after him! Below me the world
in eternal sunset, peaks flaring in the glow,
valleys tucked in slumber, and silver brooks
flowing into golden streams.
No savage mountain, no gorges then could check
my godlike flight.
And there, look!
The ocean and its sun-warmed bays
opening out to my wondering eyes!
At last the god seems near to sinking,
and a new impulse wakens in my breast:
I hurry after to drink his eternal light,
the day before me,
and behind me the night,
above me the heavens,
and below me the waves.
CHINESE: 有希望走出这片迷茫之海的人是幸福的！

when at evening the crane flaps slowly homeward
over sleepy ponds and marches.
CHINESE: 多么美的梦——直到他消失在视野之外。
可我到哪里寻找，哪里去找，

然而在我们每个人心中，都藏着这样的渴望：
升起，奔涌，翱翔而去，
当云雀在无边的蓝天高处，

当雄鹰在松林覆盖的峭壁上，
舒展双翼盘旋；
当暮色降临，鹤缓缓拍翅，
飞过沉睡的池塘和沼泽，回到家园。

SOURCE: Dark night has settled now on fields and meadows,

CHINESE: 黑夜降临在田野和草地上，

PDF 62 / UNIT 305 / verse
SOURCE: sleeping now, and the love of humankind revives,
the love of God.
Quiet there, poodle!
What’s this running about!

Youll have my best cushion.
You entertained us out there on the mountain
with your canine antics, so now
let me welcome you as my guest,
but a quiet one, you hear?
No noise!
How clear the mind becomes,
sitting here in one’s own narrow cell,
the lamp lighted and burning with a friendly glow,
the heart growing brighter with self-knowledge.
Reason once more raises its voice,
and hope blossoms.
We yearn, we long, for the springs of life,
for the very source of being.
All right now, poodle,
that’s enough whining and growling!
This beastly racket is out of tune
with the sacred harmonies gripping my soul.
We all know men who jeer at things
they don’t understand, who grumble at the
Good and the Beautiful because it embarrasses them.
Are we now to hear dogs growl, too?

CHINESE: 现在睡着了，人类的爱，

PDF 68 / UNIT 340 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES

PDF 68 / UNIT 341 / verse
SOURCE: I speak only the modest truth, sir.
And yet men,
that foolish congregation of petty microcosms,
insist on calling themselves whole entities.
As for me,
I’m a part of that Part that at first was All,
a part of the darkness that gave birth to light,
arrogant light that competes with Night
for her ancient rank and ancient space.
But it won't succeed, struggle as it may.
Light is one with objects.
Light flows from bodies,
it gives bodies beauty,
and yet bodies check it in its course.
And so, I trust, it won't be long
till light and the physical world
go down to dark destruction.
CHINESE: 我只说最朴实的事实，
先生。
然而，人类，
这个由微小的微观世界组成的愚蠢的群体，
却坚持称自己为完整的实体。
至于我，我是最初是全部的那个部分的一部分，
是诞生了光的黑暗的一部分，
傲慢的光，与黑夜争夺她古老的地位和古老的空间。
但它不会成功，尽管可能会挣扎。
光与物体合一。
光从身体流出，它赋予身体美丽，
但身体却在其过程中检查它。
因此，我相信，用不了多久，
光明和物质世界就会陷入黑暗的毁灭。

PDF 72 / UNIT 387 / verse
SOURCE: Disappear, disappear,
Drear arches above him!
Let the blue sky of heaven
Look in on us here,
The beautiful blue sky
With friendliest cheer!
Away, clouds, away,
Clouds gloomily darkling!
Behold now the little stars
Glimmering and sparkling,
Mellower suns
Shine in on us here.
Heaven’s sons, bright
In spiritual beauty,
In hovering flight
Are bending and swaying,
And souls with a passionate
Longing, aspiring,
View them, pursue them,
Soaring untiring!
And ribbons gay
Are flashing and gleaming
Where lovers stray,
Musing and dreaming,
Stray on by grove
And meadow, requiting
Love with return of love,
Life for life plighting!
Arbor on arbor,
Tendrils run rife!
Grapes in huge clusters
Piled over and over,
Under the winepress
Spurting their store.
Seething and foaming,
Wines gush into rills,
Over smooth stones
Rush from the hills,
CHINESE: 消失，消失，德雷尔在他上方拱起！

柔和的太阳照耀着我们。

PDF 75 / UNIT 410 / verse
SOURCE: That's the way!

Besides,

CHINESE: 就是这样！

CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 75 / UNIT 412 / verse
SOURCE: Whatever I wear,
what is this earth but a prison,

Too old to do anything but play,

CHINESE: 无论我穿什么，这个地球不过是一座监狱，

PDF 76 / UNIT 413 / verse
SOURCE: Do without!
Do without!
There’s the world’s advice!
There’s the song that sings eternally,
that rings in our ears, year in, year out,
hour after hour, minute by minute,
singing hoarsely till the day we die!
I wake each morning in terror,
horrified,
wishing I could shed hot, bitter tears
for the day that will give me nothing,
not one wish fulfilled,
not one satisfaction,
not one!
the day that will drag down to defeat
every faint promise of pleasure with its
constant, capricious carping,
that with a thousand petty impediments,
with life’s ugliness, its mockery, will
lay to waste my soul’s creative urge.
And then when night draws close,
and I lie on my bed,
I expect no rest, but anxiety to haunt me,
desperation, and wild, ungoverned dreams to terrify me.
The divinity that rules my heart,
that governs my powers,
can shake my soul to its depths,
but is helpless where the world is concerned.
And so,
Life is an enemy,
Being a burden,
and only Death to be wished for.
CHINESE: 不用！
不用！
世界各地都有建议！
有一首永恒的歌，在我们耳边响起，
年复一年，一小时又一小时，
一分钟又一分钟，沙哑地唱到我们死去的那一天！
每天早上我都在恐惧中醒来，
感到恐惧，
希望自己能在这一天流下滚烫、苦涩的泪水，
但那一天却什么也没有给我带来任何好处，
一个愿望都没有实现，
没有一个满足，一个都没有！
在那一天，
它将用它不断的、反复无常的吹毛求疵来挫败每一个微弱的快乐承诺，
用成千上万的小障碍，
用生活的丑陋和嘲笑，
将浪费我灵魂的创造性冲动。
然后，当夜幕降临时，
我躺在床上，我不希望休息，
但焦虑却困扰着我，
绝望和狂野的、不受控制的梦境让我感到恐惧。
掌管我心、掌管我力量的神性，
可以深深震撼我的灵魂，
但对于世界却无能为力。
所以，生是敌人，是负担，
唯有死是可求的。

PDF 77 / UNIT 424 / speaker

CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 77 / UNIT 425 / verse
SOURCE: I admit it, yes,
I was dragged from my soul’s tumult by music so sweet
it deluded that remnant of childhood in me
with echoes of a happier time.
But I curse it now,
curse it,
all that binds the soul with lures and trickery,
and with lies and flattery confines it to this fleshly cavern,
the miserable valley of despair!
I curse first the mind and its prideful self-exaltation!
I curse the dazzling stab of appearance
that assaults our senses!
I curse the dreams we dream, dreams that lie,
dreams of fame and an enduring name!
I curse property we use to flatter ourselves,
wife and child, man and plow!
I curse Mammon when with the promise of riches
he incites us to rash deeds,
or fluffs our pillows for a sensuous diversion!
CHINESE: 我承认，是的，
我被那美妙的音乐从灵魂的喧嚣中拖了出来，
它让我内心的童年残余充满了快乐时光的回声。
但我现在诅咒它，诅咒它，
所有用诱惑和诡计、用谎言和奉承把灵魂束缚在这个肉体的洞穴里、悲惨的绝望之谷的东西！
我首先诅咒心灵及其骄傲的自我抬高！
我诅咒那令人眼花缭乱的外表对我们感官的冲击！
我诅咒我们的梦想，
谎言的梦想，名誉和永恒名字的梦想！
我诅咒我们用来奉承自己、妻子和孩子、男人和犁的财产！
我咒骂玛门，因为他以财富为许诺，
煽动我们做出轻率的行为，
或者为了感官上的消遣而弄松我们的枕头！

开始！

PDF 82 / UNIT 463 / verse
SOURCE: Is the written word the only thing
with meaning for you, you pedant?
Have you never known a man who was
true to his word? Isn’t my word
enough to dispose of my days to all eternity?
Oh, I know!
You see the world flow past like a raging torrent,
and wonder why a mere promise should bind me.
But that’s the way we are.
It’s an illusion we cherish and refuse to resign.
It’s inscribed in every heart. It keeps us honest,
keeps us clear and pure spirited and responsible,
and no sacrifice in its name is too great.
But a parchment signed and sealed
is a ghost that everyone dreads.
The written word dies as the ink dries,
and leather and sealing wax tyrannize us.
So what do you want of me, you devil?
Bronze, marble, parchment, paper?
Name it.
The choice is yours.
CHINESE: 你这学究，只有写下来的文字，
对你才算有意义吗？
你难道不曾见过信守诺言的人？
我一句话，难道还不足以，
把我的一生处置到永恒？
哦，我明白了！
你看世界如狂流奔过，
便觉得区区一句许诺，怎么能束缚住我。
但人就是这样，
这幻想为我们珍爱，我们不肯舍弃。
它刻在每个人的心里，使人诚实，
使人心地清明、纯洁，敢于承担责任；
为了它，再大的牺牲也不算过分。
可一张签名盖印的羊皮纸，
却像人人畏惧的幽灵。
墨迹干时，文字也死了，
皮纸和封蜡反倒对人施行暴政。
那么，魔鬼，你究竟要什么？
青铜，大理石，羊皮纸，还是纸张？
你说吧，任你选择。

CHINESE: 你不用担心，

此外再没有别的道路。

PDF 90 / UNIT 532 / verse
SOURCE: We'll teach you, we will,
we'll pound it into your head,
that things you once did without thinking,
like eating, drinking,
things you did freely, spontaneously,
with the ease of unknowing,
you've been doing all wrong,
and that now they must be done in regimental order:
One and two and three,
and so on into infinity.
Fact is, this thought machine of ours
is like a masterpiece on a weaver’s loom.
One pedal stirs a thousand threads,
shuttles shooting back and forth,
threads flowing unseen,
and then a single stroke,
and a thousand knots are tied.
Yes, yes, and then in comes the philosopher,
sure as rain, explaining, explaining:
it had to be so, how else could it be?
First comes one,
and then comes two,
and then the three and four come due;
and if the one and two hadn’t come,
three and four would come undone!
What a round of applause that gets from the students—
and yet, not one of them ever became a weaver.
When scholars want to describe a living thing,
they must kill it first. Only then do they
have the parts in the palm of their hand,
but they’ve lost the whole—
the spirit that binds it!
Chemists call it manipulating nature,
and come out looking fools,
but don’t know why.
CHINESE: 我们会教你，我们会，
我们会把它灌输到你的脑海里，
让你知道，你曾经不假思索地做的事情，
比如吃、喝，
你自由地、自发地、轻松地不知不觉地做的事情，
你一直在做错事，而现在它们必须按团的顺序完成：
一、二、三，依此类推，
直到无穷大。
事实上，我们的这个思维机器就像织布机上的杰作。
一踏板搅动千丝，梭子来回，
丝流无形，一划，结千结。
是的，是的，然后哲学家进来了，
确定如雨，解释，解释：
它必须如此，否则怎么可能呢？
首先是一，然后是二，
然后是三和四；
如果一和二不来，三和四就会失败！
学生们响起了热烈的掌声——然而，
他们中没有一个人成为了织布工。
当学者想要描述一个生物时，
他们必须先杀死它。
只有这样，他们才掌握了部分，
但却失去了整体——束缚它的精神！
化学家称之为操纵自然，
结果看起来很傻，但不知道为什么。

according to an ironbound schedule.

and no absences;

you'll do your homework beforehand,

without consulting the text,

真的都不重要；

How?
By begging for aid from some ancient hag’s remedy?
A filthy brew will take thirty years off my back?
I pity myself if you can think of nothing better.
No, I’ve already lost hope.
Is there no natural way to this end?
Has no noble mind discovered some healing balm?
CHINESE: 我受够了！

如何？
向一些古老的女巫寻求帮助？
一杯肮脏的啤酒会让我减掉三十年的时间吗？
如果你想不出更好的办法，
我真可怜我自己。
不，我已经失去了希望。
难道没有自然的方法可以达到这个目的吗？
难道没有高尚的心灵发现了某种治愈药膏吗？

PDF 113 / UNIT 904 / verse
SOURCE: Good!
It calls for neither money, magic, nor medicine!
Get yourself out into a country field,
start hoeing,
start digging,
confine yourself and your thoughts
to a narrow sphere of reference,
eat the simple foods you raise,
live with your beasts of burden as a beast,
be proud to manure the fields with your own dung.
Believe me, there’s no better way
to keep yourself young at eighty.
CHINESE: 好！
它不需要金钱，魔法，
也不需要药物！
让自己进入乡村田地，
开始锄地，开始挖掘，
将自己和思想限制在一个狭窄的参考范围内，
吃自己种植的简单食物，
像野兽一样与驮畜一起生活，
为用自己的粪便给田地施肥而感到自豪。
相信我，
没有比这更好的方法让自己在八十岁时保持年轻了。

PDF 129 / UNIT 1129 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 129 / UNIT 1130 / stage
SOURCE: (Looking around.)
CHINESE: （环顾四周。）

PDF 129 / UNIT 1131 / verse
SOURCE: Welcome, sweet twilight,
hovering in this holy sanctuary!
Seize on my heart, sweet pain of love,
you that live and languish on the dews of hope!
About me here I feel the breath of peace,
of order, contentment.
What fullness I sense in this poverty!
What blessédness in this narrow prison!
Dear girl,
I feel your spirit of order and abundance
murmuring around me in the air,
the spirit that, like a mother, instructs you daily
to spread the clean cloth on the table and
strew the sand in patterns on the floor.
Dear hand, dear heavenly hand!
You've turned this lowly cottage into a paradise.
CHINESE: 欢迎，甜蜜的暮光，
盘旋在这神圣的圣地！
抓住我的心，爱情的甜蜜痛苦，
你在希望的露珠中生活和憔悴！
我在这里感受到了和平、秩序和满足的气息。
在这种贫穷中我感到多么充实啊！
在这狭窄的监狱里是多么幸福啊！
亲爱的女孩，
我感觉到你的秩序和丰富的精神在我周围的空气中低语，
这种精神就像母亲一样，
每天指示你在桌子上铺上干净的布，
在地板上撒上沙子。
亲爱的手，亲爱的天上的手！
你把这座简陋的小屋变成了天堂。

PDF 130 / UNIT 1132 / verse
SOURCE: And here!
CHINESE: 还有这里！

PDF 130 / UNIT 1133 / stage
SOURCE: (He lifts one of the bed curtains.)
CHINESE: （他掀起其中一张床帘。）

PDF 130 / UNIT 1134 / verse
SOURCE: What a shiver of joy comes over me!
I could linger here for hours.
It was here, blesséd Nature,
that in gentle dreams you
formed her into the angel she was born to be.
Here lay the child,
the warmth of life filling her tender body,
and here, by God’s pure and holy weaving,
the image of divinity took form.
But you!
What brings you here?
How deeply my soul is stirred!
What do you want?
Why is your heart so heavy?
Poor Faust, I no longer know you.
Am I the prisoner of some magic fragrance?
I came here driven by lust for immediate pleasure,
and now feel myself dissolved in a dream of love.
Are we the prey of every breath of air?
If she were to enter at this instant,
how you would suffer for your offense!
The swollen braggart, so petty now,
would lie here, melting, at her feet.
CHINESE: 我感到多么高兴啊！
我可以在这里逗留几个小时。
正是在这里，神圣的大自然，
您在温柔的梦中将她塑造成她与生俱来的天使。
孩子躺在这里，生命的温暖充满了她娇嫩的身体，
在这里，通过上帝纯净而神圣的编织，
神性的形象成形了。
但你！
是什么风把你吹到这里来的？
我的灵魂受到多么深刻的震撼！
你想要什么？
你的心为何如此沉重？
可怜的浮士德，我已经不认识你了。
我是某种神奇香气的囚徒吗？
我来到这里是出于对即时快乐的渴望，
现在感觉自己沉浸在爱的梦想中。
我们是每一次呼吸的空气的猎物吗？
如果她这个时候进来，
你该多受罪啊！
那个浮肿的吹牛者，
现在变得如此渺小，
将躺在这里，在她的脚下融化。

CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

CHINESE: （进入。）

PDF 131 / UNIT 1140 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

PDF 131 / UNIT 1141 / verse
SOURCE: Here's a small casket I picked up for you—
rather weighty, I'd say—
just don’t ask where.
Put it there in the clothespress. She'll see it
and go out of her mind with joy.
I've put in certain trinkets that would
win a woman of quite another stamp.
But, then, a girl’s a girl,
and a game is always a game.
CHINESE: 这是我为你挑选的一个小棺材——我想说相当重——只是不要问在哪里。
把它放在熨衣机里。
她会看到它并欣喜若狂。
我放了一些小饰品，
这些小饰品可以赢得一个完全不同的女人的青睐。
但是，女孩就是女孩，
游戏始终是游戏。

PDF 131 / UNIT 1142 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 131 / UNIT 1143 / verse
SOURCE: Should I? I don’t know—
CHINESE: 我应该吗？我不知道——

PDF 131 / UNIT 1144 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

PDF 131 / UNIT 1145 / verse
SOURCE: Now what’s the trouble?
Or perhaps you mean to keep it yourself?
In which case, I advise Your Greediness
not to waste the lovely light of day,
not to mention my time.
Oh, I do hope you're not a miser.
I rack my brains, I work my fingers to the bone—
CHINESE: 现在有什么麻烦吗？
或者也许您想自己保留它？
既然如此，我奉劝贪婪之人不要浪费美好的阳光，
更别说浪费我的时间了。
哦，我真希望你不是一个守财奴。
我绞尽脑汁，绞尽脑汁——

PDF 131 / UNIT 1146 / stage
SOURCE: (He places the casket in the clothespress and locks it again.)
CHINESE: （他把棺材放进熨衣机里，然后又锁上。）

PDF 131 / UNIT 1147 / verse
SOURCE: All right, let’s go! Now!—
all for the sake of winning this sweet young thing
to bend to your every wish;
and you stand there gloomy faced
as if you'd just entered the lecture hall
and seen there in all their drab reality
Physics and Metaphysics
staring you in the face!
Let’s go!
CHINESE: 好吧，我们走吧！
现在！
——一切都是为了赢得这个可爱的年轻人屈服于你的每一个愿望；
你站在那里，脸色阴沉，
就好像你刚刚进入演讲厅，
看到那里所有单调的现实物理和形而上学正盯着你的脸！
我们走吧！

PDF 131 / UNIT 1148 / stage

CHINESE: （众人退场。）

PDF 131 / UNIT 1149 / speaker

PDF 131 / UNIT 1151 / verse

CHINESE: 这里是多么的封闭和闷热啊。

PDF 132 / UNIT 1152 / stage
SOURCE: (She opens the window.)
CHINESE: （她打开窗户。）

PDF 132 / UNIT 1153 / verse
SOURCE: And yet it’s not all that warm outside.
I feel so—I don't know—
I wish mother would come home.
I’m shivering head to toe.
What a foolish, frightened thing I am.
CHINESE: 然而外面并不那么温暖。
我感觉——我不知道——我希望妈妈能回家​​。
我从头到脚都在发抖。
我是多么愚蠢、多么害怕啊。

PDF 132 / UNIT 1154 / stage
SOURCE: (She sings while undressing.)
CHINESE: （她一边脱衣服一边唱歌。）

PDF 132 / UNIT 1155 / verse
SOURCE: There was a king in Thulé
Faithful to the grave,
To whom his dying sweetheart
A golden goblet gave.
At every bout he drained it,
It was his fondest prize;
And every time he drank from it
Tears sprang to his eyes.
And then when Death came calling,
He tallied his towns and his lands,
Bequeathing them to his loved ones,
All but the cup in his hands.
He sat at feast with his vassals
In his castle by the sea,
In the great hall of his forebears,
For their last revelry.
The old man stood there draining
His life’s last sunset glow;
And tossed that holy goblet
Into the sea below.
He saw it fall and founder
And sink down deep in the sea.
His eyelids closed and thereafter
Not another drop drank he.
CHINESE: 图勒有一位对坟墓忠诚的国王，
他临终的爱人给了他一个金杯。
每次比赛他都会把它喝干，
这是他最喜欢的奖品；
每次他喝下它，泪水就会涌上眼眶。
然后，当死亡召唤时，
他统计了他的城镇和土地，
将它们遗赠给他所爱的人，
除了他手中的杯子。
他和他的封臣坐在海边的城堡里，
在他祖先的大厅里，
参加他们最后的狂欢。
老人站在那里，耗尽了生命中最后的夕阳余辉；
并将那神圣的高脚杯扔进下面的海里。
他看到它坠落并沉没并沉入大海深处。
他的眼睑合上，此后他就不再喝下一滴水了。

PDF 133 / UNIT 1156 / stage
SOURCE: (She opens the clothespress to put in her clothes and sees the casket of jewels.)
CHINESE: （她打开熨衣板放入衣服，看到了珠宝盒。）

PDF 133 / UNIT 1157 / verse
SOURCE: How did this pretty box get in here?
I'm certain I locked the press.
How strange!
What could be in it, I wonder?
Perhaps someone left it as a pledge
and mother made a loan on it.
Here's a small key on a ribbon.
I think I'll open it.
What’s this?
Dear God in Heaven! Look!
I've never seen the like in all my life!
Jewels!
Why, these are things a woman of the nobility
could wear on the highest holidays.
I wonder how this necklace would look on me?
Who could possibly own such splendors?
CHINESE: 这个漂亮的盒子是怎么进来的？
我确信我已经锁定了新闻机。
多么奇怪啊！
我想知道里面可能有什么？
也许有人留下它作为质押，
母亲用它贷款了。
这是丝带上的一把小钥匙。
我想我会打开它。
这是什么？
亲爱的天上的神！
看！
我一生中从未见过这样的事！
珠宝！
为什么，
这些是贵族女性在最重要的节日里可以穿的衣服。
我想知道这条项链戴在我身上会怎么样？
谁能拥有如此辉煌？

PDF 133 / UNIT 1158 / stage
SOURCE: (She puts on the necklace and looks in the mirror.)
CHINESE: （她戴上项链，照镜子。）

PDF 133 / UNIT 1159 / verse
SOURCE: If only just the earrings were mine!
Goodness, what a difference it makes!
What good are youth and beauty?
It’s all very fine if you have them,
but even so, who really cares?
People praise you half in pity.
It’s gold that counts,
money that matters.
And then there’s us poor people!
CHINESE: 要是耳环是我的就好了！
天哪，这有多么不同啊！
青春和美丽有什么用呢？
如果你拥有它们，一切都很好，
但即便如此，谁真正在乎呢？
人们半是怜悯地赞扬你。
黄金才是最重要的，
金钱才是最重要的。
还有我们这些穷人！

PDF 134 / UNIT 1170 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

PDF 134 / UNIT 1171 / verse
SOURCE: Imagine!
Those jewels I got for Gretchen?
Some priest’s just come along and snapped them up!
No sooner her mother’s eye spies them,
she goes all jittery with some secret horror.
Some nose that woman's got!
Always sniffing her prayer book.
One whiff
and she knows if something’s holy or unholy.
She knew on the spot
there wasn't much blessing in those jewels.
“Child,” cries she, “ill-gotten goods
ensnares the soul and corrupts the flesh.
We'll offer these up to God’s own Mother,
and she'll shower down manna from Heaven on us.”
CHINESE: 想象一下！
我给格蕾琴买的那些珠宝？
一些牧师刚刚过来就把它们抢走了！
当她母亲的眼睛一看到他们时，
她就因为某种秘密的恐惧而变得紧张不安。
这女人的鼻子真好！
总是嗅着她的祈祷书。
只要闻一闻，她就能知道某物是神圣的还是邪恶的。
她当场就知道这些珠宝并没有多少祝福。
 “孩子，”她喊道，
“不义之财会诱骗灵魂，
腐蚀肉体。
我们将把这些献给上帝的母亲，
她会从天堂向我们倾洒甘露。”

PDF 135 / UNIT 1172 / verse
SOURCE: Our little Gretchen pulls a long face, and thinks:
“It’s a gift horse, after all;
and how can the man nice enough to bring it,
whoever he is, be bad?”
So off her mother sends for one of those priests,
and no sooner he understands the joke
and likes what he sees, he says:
“That's the spirit!” he says.
“Who conquers his greed wins big.
The church has a marvelous digestion:
its gobbled up whole countries,
and never been glutted.
The church alone, dear ladies,
can digest ill-gotten goods.”
CHINESE: 我们的小格蕾琴板着脸，
心想：“这毕竟是一匹礼物马，
而送它来的人，不管他是谁，
怎么可能是坏人呢？”
于是，她的母亲派人去找一位牧师，
他一听懂这个笑话并喜欢他所看到的，
就说：“这就是精神！”
他说。
 “谁征服了自己的贪婪，
谁就获得了巨大的胜利。
教会有惊人的消化能力：
它吞噬了整个国家，
而且从未被过剩。
亲爱的女士们，只有教会才能消化不义之财。”

PDF 136 / UNIT 1195 / verse

but he really didn’t do right by me.
Off he goes into the wide world,
leaving me to fend for myself.
And why?
I never gave him cause.
God knows I loved him so.
CHINESE: 上帝原谅他，
我亲爱的丈夫——但他确实没有对我做任何事。
他离开了，进入了广阔的世界，
留下我自己照顾自己。
为什么？
我从来没有给过他理由。
天知道我如此爱他。

PDF 157 / UNIT 1536 / heading
SOURCE: XVII ~~ FOREST AND CAVERN
CHINESE: 第17场  森林与岩洞

PDF 157 / UNIT 1537 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 157 / UNIT 1538 / stage
SOURCE: (Alone.)
CHINESE: （独自一人。）

PDF 157 / UNIT 1539 / verse
SOURCE: Exalted spirit, you gave me all,
all I prayed for, everything.
It wasn’t for nothing
you turned your gaze on me from the flame.
You gave me glorious Nature for my kingdom,
the power to feel and enjoy her;
not merely cold and wondering visits,
but the privilege to gaze deeply into her being
as into the heart of a friend.
You parade before me endless lines
of living creatures, and teach me
to know my brothers in the silent
thicket, in air, in water.
And when the stormwind roars and rages through the forest,
and the giant fir tumbles and crashes,
crushing neighboring limbs and trunks,
and its fall echoes like thunder through the mountains,
then, oh, then you lead me to this sheltered cavern
and reveal to me the secret depths
and wonders of my heart.
And when, as now, the pale moon rises and
gently limns the landscape,
I see rising before me silvery shapes
slipping from rocky cliffs and marshy thickets
tempering the austere joy of contemplation.
CHINESE: 崇高的精神，你给了我一切，
我所祈求的一切，一切。
你把目光从火焰上移到我身上并不是无缘无故的。
你为我的王国赋予了我光荣的自然，
以及感受和享受她的力量；
不仅是冷漠而好奇的拜访，
而且是有幸深深地凝视她的存在，
就像凝视朋友的内心一样。
你在我面前展示了无数的生物，
并教我认识我在寂静的丛林中、空气中、水中的兄弟。
当暴风雨呼啸而过，
肆虐森林，巨大的冷杉翻滚、崩溃，
压碎邻近的枝干，它的倒下像雷鸣般回响在山间，
那么，哦，那么你就带我来到这个受庇护的洞穴，
向我揭示我内心的秘密深度和奇迹。
当苍白的月亮升起，
温柔地照亮大地时，
就像现在一样，我看到银色的形状在我面前升起，
从岩石悬崖和沼泽灌木丛中滑落，
缓和了沉思的严峻乐趣。

PDF 158 / UNIT 1540 / verse
SOURCE: But I know now that nothing
perfect is given to man.
You gave me this rapture that leads me nearer
and nearer to the gods,
but you gave me also the companion
I can’t do without, even though
he degrades me in my own eyes,
cold, impudent, and in a single breath
reduces your gifts to nothingness.
He fans inside me incessantly the raging passion
for that woman’s bewitching image.
So I stagger from desire to pleasure,
and having that,
languish once more for desire.
CHINESE: 但我现在知道，人类没有什么是完美的。
你给了我这种狂喜，
让我越来越接近诸神，
但你也给了我一个我离不开的伴侣，
尽管他在我眼中贬低了我，
冷酷无礼，一口气就把你的天赋降为虚无。
他不断地在我内心煽动对那个女人迷人形象的强烈热情。
因此，我从欲望到快乐摇摇欲坠，
然后，我再次因欲望而变得憔悴。

PDF 160 / UNIT 1560 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES

PDF 160 / UNIT 1561 / verse
SOURCE: Aha!
I see that doesn’t suit you!
Well, you have the right, of course,
politely, to say so.
The modest ear must never hear
what the modest heart can’t do without.
In a word, feel free to indulge yourself
in the occasional lie, but just be aware
that you can't hold out much longer.
Youre already exhausted.
This fear and trembling of yours will drive you
to distraction and madness before long.
Enough of this!
Your sweetheart sits at home feeling trapped and sad.
You're never out of her thoughts;
she loves you insanely. It was as if you
drowned her in your love's frenzy—
like a brook swollen to a torrent with melting snow.
You poured it into her heart,
but now your brook’s run dry again.
I'd suggest that instead of presiding
here in the woods as if you were somebody
you reward the poor young thing for her affection.
Time drags unbearably for her;
she stands at the window
watching clouds drift past
over the old city wall.
“If I were only a little bird” she sings
all day and half the night.
Sometimes she’s up, but mostly down,
and at times she’s wept herself dry.
She’s calm then, or appears so,
but always, always in love.
CHINESE: 啊哈！
我看这不适合你！
好吧，你当然有权利礼貌地这么说。
谦虚的耳朵绝不能听到谦虚的心所离不开的东西。
总之，偶尔撒谎是可以的，
但要注意，你不能再坚持太久了。
你已经筋疲力尽了。
你的这种恐惧和颤抖很快就会让你心烦意乱和疯狂。
够了！
你的爱人坐在家里，
感到被困和悲伤。
你永远不会脱离她的思念；
她疯狂地爱你。
就好像你把她淹没在你的爱的疯狂之中——就像一条小溪因融化的雪而膨胀成急流。
你把它倾注在她的心里，
但现在你的小溪又干了。
我建议你不要像个人一样在树林里主持会议，
而是奖励这个可怜的年轻人的感情。
时间对她来说拖得难以忍受。
她站在窗前，看着云彩飘过古老的城墙。
 “如果我只是一只小鸟就好了”她唱了一天半夜。
有时她会高兴，但大多数时候她会情绪低落，
有时她会哭干。
那时她很平静，或者看起来很平静，
但她总是、总是陷入爱河。

PDF 162 / UNIT 1581 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 162 / UNIT 1582 / verse
SOURCE: What good is heaven's joy in her arms;
what good warming myself at her breast,
when all I feel is her need, her misery, always?
What am I, what, but a homeless fugitive,
a monster who without peace or purpose
plunges like a cataract in greedy rage from
rock to rock into the abyss?
And there, at the side, in her little hut,
on her tiny Alpine meadow, dreaming
childlike dreams in her little world,
she knows nothing, nothing of the world outside.
And I, hated of God, couldn't be satisfied
with seizing the rocks and smashing them to bits—
I had to ruin her peace!
You, Hell, you, you had to have this sacrifice!
Devil, Devil, help me cut short this anguish!
Let happen what has to happen,
let it happen now!
Let her fate be my fate,
let it fall crushingly down upon me,
and hurl us to a single doom!
CHINESE: 天堂的欢乐在她的怀抱里有什么好处；
当我总是感觉到她的需要、她的痛苦时，
在她的怀里取暖有什么好处呢？
我是什么，什么，只是一个无家可归的逃亡者，
一个没有平静或目标的怪物，
像一个贪婪的愤怒中的瀑布一样从岩石到岩石坠入深渊？
而在旁边，在她的小屋里，
在她小小的高山草甸上，
她在她的小世界里做着孩子般的梦想，
她对外面的世界一无所知。
而我，憎恨上帝，
不能满足于抓住岩石并将它们砸成碎片——我必须破坏她的平静！
你，地狱，你，你必须做出这样的牺牲！
恶魔，恶魔，帮我结束这痛苦吧！
让该发生的事情发生吧，
让它现在发生！
让她的命运就是我的命运，
让她的命运压倒在我身上，
让我们陷入厄运！

PDF 163 / UNIT 1590 / verse
SOURCE: My peace is gone,
My heart is sore,
VII find it never
And never more.
Where he is not,
Is a grave to me,
The world is changed,
Ah, bitterly.
I sit, I ponder
One only thought,
My senses wander,
My brain’s distraught.
My peace is gone,
My heart is sore,
ll find it never
And never more.
I look through my window
The livelong day,
I go out to meet him,
If meet him I may.
His noble form,
His bearing high,
His mouth’s sweet smile,
His mastering eye,
CHINESE: 我的安宁已逝，
我的心在疼痛；
我再也寻不回来，
永远，永远不能。

没有他的地方，
对我便是坟墓；
整个世界都变了，
啊，变得多么痛苦。

我坐着，我思量，
心里只有一个念头；
我的神思飘荡，
我的头脑已乱透。

我的安宁已逝，
我的心在疼痛；
我再也寻不回来，
永远，永远不能。

我整日望着窗外，
想把他盼来；
我走出门去，
只为有机会与他相见。

他那高贵的身姿，
他那轩昂的气度，
他唇边甜蜜的微笑，
他摄住我心魂的目光，

PDF 164 / UNIT 1591 / verse
SOURCE: And the magic flow
Of his talk, the bliss
In the clasp of his hand,

My heart is sore,
Ill find it never
And never more.
My heart cries out,
My sad heart pines,

And kiss him, kiss him,
Night and day,
And on his kisses
Die away!
CHINESE: 他那如魔法般流转的话语，
握住他的手时的幸福，

我的安宁已逝，

我再也寻不回来，
永远，永远不能。

我的心在呼喊，
悲伤的心苦苦渴望，
要紧紧将他抱住，
要让他永远属于我。

吻他，吻他，
不分昼与夜，
然后在他的吻里，
沉醉至死！

PDF 166 / UNIT 1617 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 166 / UNIT 1618 / verse
SOURCE: Don’t mistake me, my dear.
Who can name him?
Who?
Who can testify:
“T believe in him”?
And who with life and feeling still in his body
can with any honesty say:
“T don't believe in him”?
The All-Embracing,
the All-Sustaining,
doesn’t he embrace and
sustain you, me, himself?
Doesn't the sky arch above us?
Doesn't the earth stand firm beneath us?
And the stars,
the eternal stars,
don’t they look kindly down upon us?
And when we look into each other’s eyes,
doesn’t all this press in on your heart and mind,
weaving a presence around you,
seen and unseen,
an eternal mystery?
Let if in,
fill your heart with this to overflowing,
and when it’s reached its peak,
when your bliss can grow no stronger,
then call it what you will!
Love!
Happiness!
Soul!
God!
I have no name for it.
Feeling is everything.
CHINESE: 亲爱的，别误会我。
谁能说出他的名字？
谁？
谁能作证说：
“我相信他”？
谁只要身上还有生命、还有感觉，
又能诚实地说：
“我不相信他”？
那包容一切、维系一切的存在，
不正包容着、维系着，
你、我和他自己吗？
天空不是拱覆在我们头顶？
大地不是坚实地立在我们脚下？
还有那些星辰，永恒的星辰，
不正温柔地俯视着我们吗？
当我们望进彼此的眼睛，
这一切难道不涌入你的心灵，
在你四周编织出一种存在，
可见而又不可见，
是永恒的奥秘？
让它进来，让它充满你的心，
直到洋溢，直到达到顶点，
直到你的幸福再也不能更强烈，
那时，你爱怎样称呼它，就怎样称呼它！
爱！
幸福！
灵魂！
上帝！
我无法为它取一个名字。
感觉就是一切。

SOURCE: Leave God out of this!

她的小耳朵和脑袋在夜晚隐藏在黑暗的面纱中，

光天化日之下明目张胆地到处游行，

她长得越丑，就越渴望阳光。

PDF 183 / UNIT 1848 / verse
SOURCE: As long as I’m fresh on my legs,
this knotted staff will do.
But why shorten the route?
These valleys are a labyrinth;
let’s see them first;
let’s creep our way through;
then we can climb to the rocky heights.
But look there!
That spring gushing from the mountain!
Plunging downward in its everlasting spray!
It’s a great joy seeing such sights.
Spring’s already stirring in the birches,
even the firs feel it already.
No wonder our bodies thrill to the same feeling.
CHINESE: 只要我的腿还精神，
这根打结的手杖就可以了。

这些山谷是一座迷宫；
让我们先看看他们；
让我们悄悄地过去吧；
然后我们就可以爬到岩石高处。
但看看那里！
那泉水从山上涌出来！
在永恒的浪花中向下坠落！
看到这样的景象真是太高兴了。
春天已经在白桦林中蠢蠢欲动，
就连初生者也已经感受到了。
难怪我们的身体会因同样的感觉而兴奋。

PDF 184 / UNIT 1850 / verse
SOURCE: Frankly, I feel nothing!
My body’s a wintry thing.
I'd rather be trudging
through ice and snow.
And that humpbacked moon rising reddish and late,
how sadly it climbs and gives no light,
while we bump trees and crack our shins on rocks.
Excuse me while I call on a will-o’-the-wisp.
There’s one there, shining ever so brightly!
You there! Friend!
May I have a word?
Why do you keep burning in vain?
Be so kind as to light our way up the mountain.
CHINESE: 说实话，我没什么感觉！
我的身体是一个寒冷的东西。
我宁愿在冰雪中跋涉。
那座驼背月亮升起时微红，
很晚，多么可悲的是，
它爬升时却没有发光，

在岩石上摔碎了小腿。

那里有一个，闪闪发光！
你在那里！
朋友！
我可以说句话吗？

Lovers’ sweet lamenting moan,
Loosing their happy hearts in sighs,
Voices we knew in days now flown,
When to live and love were paradise?
All that we hope for, all that we love,
Throbs in the heart and thrills in the brain,
And Echo, Echo, like the tale
Of ancient days, over hill, over dale
Reverberates the strain!
CHINESE: 小溪和小溪在苔藓和石头上奔跑。

SOURCE: How strange!
Down there, far down,
a dull reddish glow,
like dawn’s first light,
feeling its way into the deepest chasms of the abyss.
Fumes rise, vapors drift,
a cloud of mist takes sudden fire,
creeping along, a slender thread,
then gushes up like a fountain of fame and
streams its way to the valley in numberless veins,
only there, there at the narrow turn,
to unite again in a single flow.
And there, nearby, sparks, like golden sand,
are strewn about.
CHINESE: 多么奇怪啊！

一道暗红色的光芒，

摸索着进入深渊的最深处。
烟雾升腾，水蒸气飘逸，
一团薄雾突然冒出火来，
像一条细线一样爬行，
然后像名声的喷泉一样喷涌而出，
以无数的脉络流向山谷，
只有在那里，在狭窄的转弯处，
才再次汇成一股。
就在附近，火花四溅，
就像金色的沙子一样。

声音？

PDF 197 / UNIT 2009 / verse
SOURCE: In misery! In despair!
Pitiably wandering the earth for so long,

a criminal!
Confined in a dungeon,
that lovely ill-fated creature,
suffering unspeakable torments!
To have come to this!
To this!
Treacherous, abominable spirit,
how could you have done this to me?
How?
To have kept it from me?
How?
Yes! Stand there!
Stand!
Stand rolling your hellish eyes in rage!
Stand and defy me with your unendurable presence!
In prison! Ruined!
In miserable, hopeless ruin!
Abandoned to evil spirits
and to the judgment of heartless humanity!
And all this
while you lulled me with vulgar diversions,
hiding from me her growing wretchedness,
and letting her go helpless to destruction!
CHINESE: 痛苦之中！
绝望之中！
可怜在地球上流浪了这么久，
现在，现在，却被囚禁，
成为一个罪犯！
被关在地牢里，这个可爱的命运多舛的生物，
承受着难以言喻的折磨！
竟然到了这个地步！
到这个！
奸诈可恶的鬼魂，你怎么能这样对我？
如何？
瞒着我？
如何？
是的！
站在那里！
站立！
站起来，愤怒地转动你地狱般的眼睛！
站起来用你难以忍受的存在来反抗我！
在监狱里！
毁了！
陷入悲惨、绝望的毁灭之中！
被邪恶的灵魂和无情的人类的审判所抛弃！
而在这整个过程中，
你却用粗俗的消遣来哄骗我，
向我隐瞒她日益增长的不幸，
让她无助地走向毁灭！

PDF 198 / UNIT 2013 / verse
SOURCE: Beast! Detestable monster!—
Transform him, transform this reptile,
oh, you, you Infinite Spirit of Earth!
Change him into the dog that he once was,
cavorting on evening walks,
rolling at the feet of the unsuspecting wanderer,
bounding onto his shoulders when he falls.
Change him back into his favorite form,
to grovel before me in the sand on his belly,
so I can trample him,
trample the depraved creature!
“Not the first”!
O grief,
grief not to be grasped
by human soul,
that more than one creature
has reached this depth
of misery;
that that first failed
to atone for all others
in her death agony
before the eyes of the
ever-forgiving God!
The misery of this one,
her,
cuts me to the marrow of my being!
And you stand there grinning coolly
across the doom of thousands.
CHINESE: 野兽！
可憎的怪物！
——改造他，改造这个爬行动物，
哦，你，你无限的大地之灵！
把他变成以前的狗，
在晚上散步时嬉戏，
在毫无戒心的流浪者脚下打滚，
在他跌倒时跳到他的肩膀上。
把他变回他最喜欢的样子，
趴在我面前，趴在沙子里，
让我践踏他，践踏这个堕落的生物！
 “不是第一个”！
噢，悲痛，人类灵魂无法理解的悲痛，
不止一个生物已经达到了如此悲惨的程度；
在永远宽恕的上帝面前，
她在死亡的痛苦中首先未能为所有其他人赎罪！
这个人，她的痛苦，
让我痛彻心扉！
而你站在那里，面对成千上万的厄运，
冷酷地笑着。

PDF 214 / UNIT 2198 / heading
SOURCE: XXVIII ~~ A PLEASANT LANDSCAPE, TWILIGHT
CHINESE: 第28场  幽美的风景 朦胧微光

PDF 214 / UNIT 2199 / stage
SOURCE: FAUST, bedded on a flowery meadow, exhausted, restless, trying to sleep.
Graceful little SPIRITS hover over him in a circle.
ARIEL sings accompanied by Aeolian harps.
CHINESE: 浮士德躺在鲜花盛开的草地上，疲惫不堪，焦躁不安，想睡觉。优雅的小精灵在他上方盘旋一圈。 阿里埃尔在风琴的伴奏下歌唱。

PDF 214 / UNIT 2200 / speaker
SOURCE: ARIEL
CHINESE: 阿里埃尔

PDF 214 / UNIT 2201 / verse
SOURCE: When the rain of new spring blossoms
Blesses every hill and glen,
When green meadows, bright with showers,
Gladden all the sons of men,
Elvin creatures, great in power,
Run to help where help they can,
Whether he be good or evil,
They sorrow for the luckless man.
You spirits, elves,
who hover in broad circles above his head,
show him now the noble nature of elves.
Ease his heart,
root out the fierce strife that tears him,
withdraw the bitter, burning arrows
of self reproach and purge his soul
of the horror he has known.
Four are the watches of the night:
fill them now with gentle kindness
and no delay.
Lower his head first on a cool pillow and
bathe him in the dew of Lethe’s waters.
His body, now cramped and rigid,
will gather strength as he relaxes and
rests to meet the new day.
Perform your fairest task as elves,
restore him to the sacred light.
CHINESE: 当新春的花雨，
祝福每一道山谷、每一座山丘，
当绿草地在雨中闪耀，
使世上所有的人欢喜，
强大的小精灵，
便赶到每一个需要帮助的地方；
不论那人是善是恶，
他们都怜悯不幸的人。
你们这些精灵、小仙子，
在他头顶宽阔地盘旋，
此刻让他见识精灵高贵的本性。
使他的心得到安慰，
拔除撕裂他的猛烈争斗，
拔去自责那灼热苦痛的箭，
涤净他的灵魂，
洗去他经历过的恐怖。
一夜分为四更，
快把温柔的善意，
注满每一更的时辰。
先让他的头倚上清凉的枕，
用勒忒河的露水，为他沐浴。
他紧缩僵硬的身体，
将在松弛中重获力量，
休息好了，迎接新的一天。
履行你们精灵最美好的职责，
使他重返神圣的光明。

PDF 215 / UNIT 2202 / speaker
SOURCE: CHORUS OF ELVES
CHINESE: 小精灵合唱队

PDF 215 / UNIT 2203 / stage
SOURCE: (Alternately solo, duo, and in chorus.)
CHINESE: （交替独奏、二重奏和合唱。）

PDF 215 / UNIT 2204 / verse
SOURCE: Serenade
When across the soft-green meadows
Warm and gentle breezes play,
Closing round in misty shadows
Softly falls the close of day;
Whispers gently peace to mortals,
Rocks the heart to childlike rest,
Closes up the daylight’s portals
To those wearied eyes unblessed.
Notturno
Now the night is deeply darkling,
Hallowed star joins hallowed star,
Lights of power, or faintly sparkling,
Glimmer near, and gleam afar;
Glimmer in the lake so tender,
Gleam in heaven’s vault profound;
The bright moon reigns in full-orbed splendor,
Making perfect peace abound.
Mattutino
The hours of the night have vanished,
Joy and grief are passed away.
Feel the promise! Pain is banished!
Trust the new and hopeful day.
Vales grow green, hills steep and steeper,
Shadows deepen thick with leaves,
And the harvest to the reaper
In long silvery billows heaves.
Réveille
Wishes, win them, without number,
Turn and face the dawning day,
You are wrapped in sleep’s light slumber,
Cast it off and greet the day!
Be bold where others fear to tread,
Seize the day for all its worth,
CHINESE: Serenade（小夜曲）
温暖柔和的风，
掠过嫩绿的草地，
暮色悄然降落，
四周合拢迷雾的阴影；
轻轻向凡人低语安宁，
摇抚他的心，赐他孩童般的休憩，
为那疲惫而不幸的双眼，
关上白昼的门户。

Notturno（夜曲）
如今夜色深深，
神圣的星辰与星辰相连；
有的光辉强盛，有的微微闪烁，
近处幽明，远处灿然；
温柔地映在湖中，
照入深邃的苍穹；
皓月以圆满的光辉主宰夜空，
使彻底的安宁充满四方。

Mattutino（晨曲）
夜里的时辰已经逝去，
欢乐与悲伤一同过去。
感受这许诺！痛苦已经驱散！
相信充满希望的新一天。
山谷转绿，山峦层层陡起，
浓叶使树荫更深，
成熟的庄稼如长长的银浪，
向收割的人起伏。

Réveille（唤醒曲）
无尽的愿望，去把它们赢来！
转过身，面对破晓的白昼；
你还裹在浅浅的睡眠中，
抛开它，迎接新的一天！
别人不敢涉足之处，你要勇敢前往，
抓住白昼，尽取它的价值，

PDF 216 / UNIT 2207 / speaker
SOURCE: ARIEL
CHINESE: 阿里埃尔

PDF 216 / UNIT 2208 / verse
SOURCE: Harken! Hark! The storm of hours!
Pealing out to spirit ears,
Rejoice! Another day is ours!
Rejoice! Another dawn appears!
Granite gates are crashing, crashing,
Phoebus’s car wheels rattling, clashing—
What clangor announces the rising sun!
Drums and trumpets pealing clear,
Dazzling eye and stunning ear!
Away! Our elfin reign is done.
Into flowery chalice creep,
Rest in quiet, deep in sleep,
Or under rock, or under cleft;
If you hear it, you are deaf.
CHINESE: 听！听啊！时辰的风暴！
在精灵耳中轰然鸣响；
欢欣吧！新的一天属于我们！
欢欣吧！又一道曙光显现！
花岗岩的大门砰然开启，
福玻斯的车轮铿锵轰鸣——
这震天声响宣告太阳升起！
鼓与号角响彻高空，
炫目，震耳！
快走！精灵的统治已经结束。
爬进花朵的杯盏，
安静休息，沉沉入梦；
或躲入岩石之下、岩缝之中；
倘若听见这巨响，你们便会耳聋。

PDF 216 / UNIT 2209 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 216 / UNIT 2210 / verse
SOURCE: Life’s pulses beat in me now with fresh vitality,
ready to greet the dawn’s ethereal day.
Earth, great earth,
you were loyal to me in the night,
and now you, too,
revived anew,
already infuse me with a grand desire,
rousing in me a powerful resolve:
to strive and ever to strive to the pinnacle of Being.
The world is unlocked to view
in the dawn’s first dim light;
the woods are alive and ring with a thousand voices;
and valleys high and low are shrouded in mist
ull the clear light of heaven descends to their depths.
And from the misty haze
boughs and branches waken
CHINESE: 生命的脉搏现在带着新鲜的活力在我体内跳动，
准备好迎接黎明的空灵的一天。
大地，伟大的大地，
你在黑夜里忠于我，
现在你也重新复活，
已经给我注入了伟大的愿望，
激起了我强大的决心：
奋斗并永远奋斗到存在的顶峰。
黎明的第一缕微光中，
世界已被解锁；
树林生机勃勃，回响着一千种声音；
高低不一的山谷都笼罩在薄雾之中，
而天上的清光却降到了它们的深处。
树枝从薄雾中苏醒

PDF 217 / UNIT 2211 / verse
SOURCE: and burst from the fragrant abyss where they rest.
Colors, too, colors upon colors,
emerge clear as day from the fertile ground
where every flower and leaf
is wet with pearls of dew.
The world about me has become a Paradise!
Look up!
Up there!
Already mighty peaks announce
that most solemn of hours!
They are the first to enjoy the eternal light
that later makes its way down to us.
And now the upper meadows are agleam
with brilliant clarity in all their greenness,
and step by step the slow descent is concluded—
and out comes the sun!
Ah, and dazed already by its radiance,
I turn aside my eyes from his painful glare.
Is it ever otherwise?
When with a keen desire
we have struggled our way up to the supreme moment,
and find the gates of fulfillment flung wide,
we are met by a burst of flame from eternal depths
that leaves us standing confounded.
Our aim was to light life’s torch,
and an ocean surge of fire engulfs us.
And what a fire it is!
Is it love,
is it hate,
this fire that swirls around us,
alternating pain and joy beyond endurance,
driving us back to earth again
to hide in the misty veils of early youth?
Well—
then let the sun remain at my back.
I look and I see with greater and greater delight
CHINESE: 从他们休息的芬芳深渊中冲出。
颜色也是如此，颜色上的颜色，
从肥沃的土地上清晰地浮现出来，
那里的每一朵花和叶子都沾满了珍珠般的露珠。
我周围的世界变成了天堂！
抬头！
上面！
雄伟的山峰已经宣告了最庄严的时刻！
他们是第一个享受永恒之光的人，
而永恒之光后来又降临到我们身上。
现在，上面的草地在绿色中闪烁着明亮的光芒，
缓慢的下降一步一步结束了——太阳出来了！
啊，我已经被它的光芒弄晕了，
我把目光从他痛苦的目光中移开。
难道有别的情况吗？
当我们带着强烈的愿望奋力到达至高无上的时刻，
并发现实现之门敞开时，
我们会遇到来自永恒深处的一阵火焰，
让我们感到困惑。
我们的目标是点燃生命的火炬，
然后汹涌的火海吞没了我们。
那是一场多么大的火灾啊！
是爱，是恨，这团在我们周围盘旋的火焰，
令人难以忍受的痛苦和欢乐交替着，
驱使我们再次回到尘世，
躲在青春的薄雾里？
好吧——那就让阳光留在我的背上吧。
我越看越高兴

PDF 218 / UNIT 2212 / verse
SOURCE: this roaring waterfall gushing from its rocky crag.
Plunging from level to level,
it shatters into a thousand streams,
and then into thousands more, sending up clouds
of spray heaven high. And then,
gloriously rising from this storm of commotion,
the tinted arch of the rainbow’s changing permanence—
now sharply etched,
now melting into air—
scatters fragrant showers all around.
In this I see a mirror of human striving.
Consider, and it’s easily understood:
Life is not light, but the refracted color.
CHINESE: 这座咆哮的瀑布从岩石峭壁中喷涌而出。
它从一个层面跌落到另一个层面，
破碎成一千条溪流，
然后又变成数千条溪流，
高高地喷射出云朵。
然后，从这场骚乱的风暴中光荣地升起，
彩虹的持久变化的彩色拱门——现在被尖锐地蚀刻，
现在融化在空气中——向四周散落芬芳的阵雨。
在此我看到了人类奋斗的一面镜子。
想一想，这很容易理解：
生命不是光，而是折射的颜色。

PDF 220 / UNIT 2232 / verse
SOURCE: You are gathered here together
under an auspicious star that promises
prosperity and happiness.
But why, I ask you, why at just this time,
when we would free ourselves of cares and troubles,
when we long to pull on mummers’ masks for Carnival
and do nothing so much as enjoy ourselves—
why, then, are we met,
why do we torment ourselves
with affairs of state?
But since you said it had to be,
well, then, let it be!
CHINESE: 你们聚集在一颗预示着繁荣和幸福的吉祥星下。
但我问你，为什么在这个时候，
当我们要摆脱忧虑和烦恼的时候，
当我们渴望戴上小丑面具参加狂欢节，
除了享受自己之外什么也不做的时候——那么，
为什么我们会相遇，
为什么我们要用国家事务来折磨自己呢？
不过既然你都这么说了，
那好吧，那就这样吧！

PDF 220 / UNIT 2233 / speaker
SOURCE: CHANCELLOR
CHINESE: 宰相

PDF 220 / UNIT 2234 / verse
SOURCE: Justice,
that highest of virtues,
rests on the Emperor's head like a halo.
He alone has the power to exercise it.
What all men love, demand, desire,
what they desperately need,
it is his prerogative to give them,
his subjects, his people.
Ah, but what good is intelligence to the mind,
goodness to the heart,
readiness to the hand,
when the body politic rages with fever,
and one single evil spawns a whole host of evils?
One glance from this height across your vast empire, Sire,
will reveal a nightmare
in which Deformity reigns among deformities,
where Lawlessness rules in place of law,
and Error multiplies and populates the earth.
One man steals cattle, another a neighbor's wife,
and a third runs off with the altar’s
chalice, candlestick, and crucifix,
and they end up boasting about it for years
without a scratch to limb or reputation.
CHINESE: 正义是最高的美德，
它像光环一样戴在皇帝的头上。
只有他有权力行使它。
所有人所喜爱、要求、渴望的东西，
以及他们迫切需要的东西，
他有特权给予他们、他的臣民、他的人民。
啊，但是，当政治体因发烧而肆虐，
而一种邪恶会滋生出一大堆邪恶时，
聪明对于头脑、善良对于心灵、准备就绪又有什么好处呢？
陛下，从这个高度看一眼您广阔的帝国，
就会发现一场噩梦，
其中畸形统治着畸形，
无法无天取代了法律，
错误在地球上繁殖和蔓延。
一个人偷了牛，另一个人偷了邻居的妻子，
还有一个人偷走了祭坛上的圣杯、烛台和十字架，
最后他们吹嘘了很多年，
没有伤到四肢，也没有名声大噪。

PDF 221 / UNIT 2235 / verse
SOURCE: Plaintiffs crowd into courtrooms
where the judge sits in state on his well-stuffed pillow,
while in the streets seething angry mobs
threaten rebellion.
A man supported by accomplices worse than he
has the audacity to brag of his heinous crime,
his infamy, whereas when innocence
has only itself to defend it
you will always hear the verdict “Guilty!”
And what happens in consequence?
The world falls apart,
decency is outraged,
and how is a spirit to develop
that will lead us to what is right and proper?
In the end, the worthy man has no choice
but to bow to flattery and bribery,
and the judge in his inability to punish
must join forces with the criminal.
For all the blackness of the picture I’ve painted,
I would prefer to have painted it even darker.
CHINESE: 原告挤进法庭，法官端坐在塞满枕头的枕头上，
而街上愤怒的暴民则威胁要叛乱。
一个在比他更糟糕的同伙支持下的人，
竟然厚颜无耻地吹嘘自己的滔天罪行、臭名昭著，
而当清白只能靠自己来捍卫时，
你总会听到“有罪！”
的判决。
结果会发生什么？
世界分崩离析，正派被侵犯，
如何培养一种精神来引导我们走向正确和正确的道路呢？
最终，贤人只能屈服于阿谀奉承和贿赂，
法官无力惩罚，只能与罪犯联手。
尽管我画的图画很黑，
但我宁愿把它画得更暗。

SOURCE: So much for allies!

SOURCE: Ah, and the things / have to contend with!
Day after day we do all we can to save,

Wild boar, venison, rabbit, turkey, chicken,

secured revenue,

the best vineyards, the best vintages,

The City Council, too, has had to
tap its stocks. They grab any mug or bowl
to swill ic down, and the next thing you know
they're under the table.
So now I pay bills and wages with money from loans
that eat up the future before it’s arrived.
Our pigs haven't time to fatten,
the pillow’s been pawned that we lay our heads on,
and even the bread on the table is mortgaged.
CHINESE: 啊，还有必须应对的事情！

这些钱在未来到来之前就吞噬了未来。
我们的猪还没来得及养肥，
我们枕的枕头都被典当了，
连桌上的面包也被典当了。

PDF 228 / UNIT 2290 / verse
SOURCE: There they stand, wide-eyed and gaping,

the time will come when their soles will itch
and their steady tread take a tumble.
It’s then you will feel
the eternal hidden power of Nature.
From deep in subterranean depths
a trace of life works its way to the surface.
When your every limb is a-tremble,
when a certain place has an uncanny air,
don’t hesitate then, but take up shovel and pick
and dig, for that’s the spot, that’s where it is,
that’s where your treasure lies hidden!
CHINESE: 他们站在那里，睁大眼睛，

他们稳定的脚步会翻倒。
那时你就会感受到大自然永恒的隐藏力量。
生命的痕迹从地下深处一直延伸到地表。

PDF 236 / UNIT 2340 / verse
SOURCE: Give place! Make space!
Trees we fell,
They fall pell-mell,
Down as we tear them,
Down to the dell!
Crunching, crashing,
Straining and lashing,
They crash in the dust,
Fall, for they must!
Down to the dust!
And off as we bear them
They push and they thrust.
Say this in our praise
To the end of our days,
Were there no rough men
To work in the land,
Where, tell me, then,
Would you fine folks stand?
This is the truth,
Never forget it,
If we didn’t sweat it,
How would you stretch
At your ease,
if you please?
You would all freeze!
CHINESE: 让位！

嘎吱作响，撞击，拉紧和鞭打，

因为他们必须坠落！
归于尘土！
当我们背着它们离开时，
它们又推又推。
以此来表达我们的赞美，
直到我们生命的终结，
如果没有粗野的人在这片土地上工作，
那么请告诉我，你们这些好人会站在哪里吗？
这是事实，永远不要忘记，
如果我们不付出汗水，
如果你愿意的话，你会如何轻松地伸展身体？
你们都会冻僵的！

CHINESE: 并看着这一切发生！

SOURCE: (Surrounding great PAN.)

PDF 255 / UNIT 2484 / verse
SOURCE: Great Pan is here!
Great Pan is come!
Great Pan who is All!

and he wishes us all to be merry.
He would be watchful,
would be ever attentive,
beneath the blue of heaven's arch,
except that brooklets ripple past,
and gentle, soft breezes caress him to drowse.
And when at noon he sleeps,
no leaf stirs,
and in the silent, motionless air
the healthy scent of Nature hovers,
and no nymph dare stay awake,
but falls asleep just where she stands.
Suddenly then his voice cries out
like lightning’s crash, like the roaring sea,
and panic descends on even the mightiest,
armies scatter and heroes tremble.
CHINESE: 大锅来了！

他希望我们大家都快乐。
在蓝色的天堂拱门下，
他会保持警惕，永远专心，
只有小溪潺潺流过，
轻柔的微风抚摸着他，
让他入睡。
当他中午睡着的时候，
没有树叶摇动，

没有哪个仙女敢保持清醒，
而是在她站着的地方睡着了。
忽然，他的声音大喊，
如雷电轰鸣，如大海咆哮，
连强者都惊慌失措，
军队溃散，英雄颤抖。

PDF 256 / UNIT 2486 / speaker
SOURCE: DEPUTATION OF GNOMES TO GREAT PAN
CHINESE: 晋见大神潘的地精代表团

PDF 256 / UNIT 2487 / verse
SOURCE: Where rich and brightly shining ore
Threads its way through mountain core,
Only the divining rod can

You give to man your treasures bright.
But now, nearby, we've found a treasure
Of so great wealth it’s beyond measure;
A source so grand we never thought
To see its like from deep earth brought.
It’s yours to bring this to fulfillment,
Lord, to save it from concealment;
Treasure in the Great Pan’s hand
Brings benefit to every land.
CHINESE: 丰富而闪亮的矿石穿过山心，

我们生活在地下黑暗的洞穴里，
就像穴居人一样，那里没有太阳，

其财富之巨大，难以估量；
我们从未想过看到它来自地球深处的源头如此宏伟。
主啊，你有责任使这一切得以实现，
将其从隐藏中拯救出来；
大盘手中的宝藏，造福四方土地。

PDF 257 / UNIT 2494 / verse

How can he be so unsuspecting?

Whose beard was it?

His hand hides it so none can see.
Ah, but what a calamity now!
The beard aflame flies back up

of followers escapes!
Even as they slap and beat at the flames,
new flames are fanned,
a new blaze begun! Engulfed in fire,
a group of maskers burns to death!
But what’s this I hear
spread by word of mouth from ear to ear?
O night, unhappy night,

Tomorrow will bring what no one wants to hear!

O God, if only it weren't true!

curse all those who misled him,
CHINESE: 然后再次沉入最深处，

PDF 259 / UNIT 2505 / verse

PDF 260 / UNIT 2508 / verse

天堂不能更欢乐了！

PDF 262 / UNIT 2537 / verse
SOURCE: Consider, Sire! Remember!

and with a few strokes of the pen
crown this grand festivity
with salvation for your people!”
And sign it you did, Sire, and in the night
our nimble conjurors ran off thousands.
And to allow everyone without delay
to share in the benefit,
we at once stamped the whole series
Tens, thirties, fifties, hundreds
are now ready to hand.
You can't imagine the pleasure your subjects feel!
Take a look at your city.
It was dead and rotting before,
but now it’s alive and teeming with pleasure seekers!
Your name has always meant happiness,
but never has it been regarded with such devotion.
The alphabet lost its value last night,
for in this signature all men find happiness!
CHINESE: 请考虑一下，陛下！

签署这张纸条，用笔轻轻几笔，
为你的人民带来救赎，
为这个盛大的节日加冕！”
陛下，您签署了，一夜之间，
我们敏捷的魔术师就消灭了数千人。
为了让每个人都能立即分享利益，
我们立即在整个系列上盖章，
十，三十，五十，数百现在已准备就绪。

以前它已经死了、腐烂了，
但现在却又活过来了，
充满了寻欢作乐的人！
你的名字一直意味着幸福，
但从未被如此虔诚地对待。
字母表昨晚失去了它的价值，
因为在这个签名中，
所有的人都找到了幸福！

Clothiers cut their cloth and tailors sew.
Wine flows like water in taverns
toasting the Emperor.
Food’s a-frying and baking,
and everywhere
there’s the clatter of dishes.
CHINESE: 即使我们愿意，我们也永远无法恢复这些笔记。

PDF 263 / UNIT 2543 / verse
SOURCE: Walk alone some evening along the terraces,

wondering if you have one of those special notes

when a banknote’s place is snug in a lover’s breast,
close beside an amorous billet-doux?

and the soldier with a lighter money belt
will swerve more swiftly.
CHINESE: 某个晚上，你独自沿着露台散步，

微笑着，

PDF 264 / UNIT 2548 / verse

You auction off a golden chain or goblet,

will put to shame all the doubters who called us fools.

As a result, the Empire will never be anything
but well supplied with jewels, gold, and paper.
CHINESE: 纸币代替黄金和珍珠确实是最方便的。

如果这没有什么好处，
你就出去挖一会儿。

纸币迅速摊销，

一旦习惯了这一点，
没有人会想要它。

SOURCE: A difficult case, I admit.

You must take this piece of charcoal

With the charcoal then
you'll put a mark on him somewhere—
arm, coat, shoulder, wherever,
and at once he'll suffer the sweet pangs of remorse.
Immediately, then, you must swallow the charcoal
and let neither wine nor water touch your lips.
This very night he'll be sighing at your door.
CHINESE: 我承认，这是一个困难的案例。

你必须拿着这块木炭，

无论什么地方，他立刻就会遭受悔恨的甜蜜痛苦。
然后，你必须立即吞下木炭，
不要让酒或水接触你的嘴唇。
今晚他就会在你家门口叹息。

PDF 281 / UNIT 2767 / verse
SOURCE: His glowing key no sooner touches the censer

with measured step,
a beautiful youth steps forth.
Here I end my task, for I needn't name him.
Who could fail to know the charming Paris.
CHINESE: 他发光的钥匙一接触香炉，

伸展、膨胀、凝结、缠绕、分离、结合。

一切，柱子，三字形，
环；

我的任务到此结束，
因为我不必说出他的名字。
谁能不知道迷人的帕里斯。

PDF 284 / UNIT 2816 / speaker

PDF 284 / UNIT 2817 / verse
SOURCE: Do I still have eyes?
Or is it deep in my soul
I see Beauty’s wellspring in full stream?
My quest of terror has brought me a blissful reward.
How without meaning,
how closed the world was to me!
But my priesthood has made it desirable,
well founded, enduring!
May the breath of life desert me
if I resign myself to living without you.
The fair form that bewitched me long ago
in a magic reflection
was a mere mocking counterfeit of this Beauty.
I pledge to you herewith, as tribute due,
the sum of my strength,
the quintessence of passion,
affection, love, adoration, madness!
CHINESE: 我还有眼睛吗？

如果我屈从于没有你的生活，
愿生命的呼吸抛弃我。
很久以前，
在神奇的倒影中令我着迷的美丽形象只不过是对这位美丽的嘲讽赝品。
我在此向你保证，我的力量之和，
激情、感情、爱、崇拜和疯狂的精髓，
作为应有的敬意！
