每天都在增加智慧的储备。
全世界都渴望知识，

PDF 293 / UNIT 2921 / verse
SOURCE: throngs to the lectures of this semaphore of thinkers,
who, like St. Peter, wields the keys
that unlock the upper and lower worlds.
There’s no one alive can hold a candle to him.
The brilliance and glow of his fame and glory
have even cast a shadow on the name of Faust.
Professor Wagner, yes,
the only true discoverer.
CHINESE: 成群涌去，听这位思想界的旗手讲课；
他如圣彼得，执掌着，
开启上下两个世界的钥匙。
活着的人，谁也不能与他相比。
他的声望与荣耀如此灿烂，
连浮士德的名字，也被遮在阴影里。
瓦格纳教授，没错，
唯一真正的发现者。

PDF 293 / UNIT 2922 / speaker
SOURCE: FAMULUS
CHINESE: 助手

PDF 293 / UNIT 2923 / verse
SOURCE: Pardon, Most Reverend Sir,
if I dare to contradict you,
but it’s not that way at all.
He’s a modest man, my master,
a man who never reconciled himself
to the mysterious disappearance
of that exalted man.
He hopes and prays daily for solace
and salvation from his return.
This room, just as in that great man’s day,
is untouched, just as he left it,
awaiting its own master.
I scarcely dared to enter it, sir.
What is it must be written in the stars
to make the very walls shake with terror,
and the doorposts to tremble?
Even the bolts sprang back,
or you couldn't have entered.
CHINESE: 请原谅，尊敬的阁下，
如果我敢反驳您的话，
但事实并非如此。
我的主人，他是一个谦虚的人，
一个对那位尊贵的人神秘失踪从来不甘心的人。
他每天都希望并祈祷他的归来能得到安慰和拯救。
这个房间，就像那位伟人的时代一样，
没有受到任何影响，
就像他离开时一样，
等待着它自己的主人。
我几乎不敢进去，先生。
星星中必须写下什么才能让墙壁因恐惧而颤抖，
让门柱颤抖？
连门闩都弹回来了，
不然你根本进不去。

PDF 294 / UNIT 2933 / speaker
SOURCE: BACCALAUREATE
CHINESE: 学士

PDF 294 / UNIT 2934 / stage
SOURCE: (Storming down the corridor.)
CHINESE: （冲过走廊。）

PDF 294 / UNIT 2935 / verse
SOURCE: What’s this?
Doors and gates thrown wide open?
About time, too! Let in some air!
Let us breathe for a change,
rather than stifle in this decay,
living like corpses, rotting, wasting away
in this moldy heap of rubbish!
These walls are leaning and sagging,
about to collapse, and either I get out of here
fast or be buried by them.
I risk as much as the next,
but no one’s dragging me farther into this house.
But wait.
Yes, I’ve been here before!
I have! Years ago.
I was a freshman.
CHINESE: 这是什么？
门和大门全开？
也到时候了！
让一些空气进来！
让我们呼吸改变，而不是在这腐烂中窒息，
像尸体一样活着，腐烂，
在这发霉的垃圾堆里消瘦！
这些墙壁倾斜、下垂，
即将倒塌，我要么赶紧离开这里，
要么被它们埋掉。
我和下一个一样冒着同样的风险，
但没有人把我拖进这所房子。
但是等等。
是的，我以前来过这里！
我有！
几年前。
我当时是一年级新生。

PDF 295 / UNIT 2936 / verse
SOURCE: Timid little mouse, scared of my own shadow.
God, and naive! I trusted them—
those gray-bearded old fools and their eternal babble!
They lied is what they did,
lied to me,
lied right out of their crusty ancient books!
Teaching they called it!
Telling me what they knew, they said!
When what they knew they didn’t themselves believe!
Wasting their lives and mine!
But what's that?
Someone back in that cell—
sitting in the gloom.
Coming closer, I can’t believe what I’m seeing.
Still sitting there, just as I left him,
still wrapped in that shaggy fleece.
I thought at the time he was terribly wise;
that, of course, was because I wasn’t.
This time that won't be a problem.
Let’s have at him then!
Well, now, old gentleman
(if your bald, bowed head
is still unmoistened by Lethe’s turgid stream),
you may recognize a former student,
who meanwhile has outgrown the master’s rod.
I find you just as I left you.
As for myself,
I return quite another man.
CHINESE: 胆小的小老鼠，害怕自己的影子。
天啊，天真！
我信任他们——那些白胡子老傻瓜和他们永远的胡言乱语！
他们撒谎就是他们所做的，
对我撒谎，完全是从他们的古书上撒的谎！
他们称之为教学！
他们说，告诉我他们所知道的！
当他们知道的时候，
他们自己却不相信！
浪费他们的生命和我的生命！
但那是什么？
有人回到那间牢房里——坐在黑暗中。
走近了，我简直不敢相信自己所看到的。
仍然坐在那里，就像我离开他一样，
仍然裹着那件毛茸茸的羊毛。
我当时觉得他非常聪明。
当然，那是因为我不是。
这次就不会有问题了。
那我们就来对付他吧！
好吧，现在，
老先生（如果你低着的秃头还没有被勒忒河的湍急的溪水浸湿的话），
你可能会认出一位以前的学生，
而他此时已经不再适合老师的杖了。
我找到你就像我离开你一样。
至于我自己，我完全变了一个人。

PDF 298 / UNIT 2968 / speaker

CHINESE: 学士

PDF 298 / UNIT 2969 / verse
SOURCE: How presumptuous!
At a time of life when you're nothing,
when you don’ count,
to go on pretending you're somebody!
Life and blood are one and the same,
everything in life depends on it, and
where is it fresher and more vital,
where does it surge with primal force more
vigorously than in the body of youth?
It’s the source of all creativity:
new life out of life.
It’s a time when everything is on the move;
things get done; when the weak falter,
they're pushed aside, and the fit,
the vigorous, take their place.
While we have conquered half the world with ideas,
what have you done
but nod, ponder, dream, deliberate?
Plans, always plans!
No doubt about it,
age is a cold fever,
an ague of whims and impotent fantasies.
You've left thirty behind, you say?
You're as good as dead.
The best advice would be to kill you all off.
CHINESE: 多么放肆啊！
当你在人生中一无所有、无足轻重的时候，
继续假装自己很重要！
生命与血液是一体的，
生命的一切都依赖于它，
哪里比年轻时的身体更鲜活、更生机勃勃、更澎湃元力？
它是所有创造力的源泉：
从生活中获得新的生命。
这是一个一切都在运转的时期；
事情完成了；
当弱者动摇时，他们就会被推到一边，
强健的、精力充沛的人就会取代他们的位置。
当我们用思想征服了半个世界时，
除了点头、沉思、梦想、深思熟虑之外，
你还做了什么？
计划，永远是计划！
毫无疑问，年龄是一种感冒发烧，
是一种突发奇想和无力的幻想。
你说你已经落后了三十个人？
你就跟死了一样。
最好的建议就是把你们都杀掉。

PDF 299 / UNIT 2977 / speaker

CHINESE: 学士

PDF 299 / UNIT 2978 / verse
SOURCE: This is the noblest mission of the young!
The world was not, until I gave it being.
I urged the sun to rise out of the sea.
It was I who set the moon on its changing course.
As I passed by the earth put on its finest,
grew green and blossomed to meet me.
At my sign, the stars in all their splendor
spread out in the heavens on that first night.
Who but I delivered you from the narrow,
literal-minded philistine?
But I’m free, free as the voice inside me,
and joyous to be so, to follow my inner light
and move as swiftly as thought can carry me,
day before me, and behind me night.
CHINESE: 这是年轻人最崇高的使命！
世界并不存在，直到我创造了它。
我催促太阳从海里升起。
是我让月亮改变了轨迹。
当我经过时，大地披上了最美的色彩，
绿油油的，鲜花盛开迎接我。
在我的星座，第一个夜晚，
繁星在天空中绽放出绚丽的光芒。
除了我之外，
谁能把你从狭隘的、只注重字面意义的市侩中拯救出来呢？
但我是自由的，就像我内心的声音一样自由，
而且很高兴能够如此，
跟随我内心的光芒，
以思想所能携带的最快速度移动，
白天在我之前，在我后面黑夜。

CHINESE: 那可怕的铃声响起，

不会再有！
哦，上帝，是什么让门嘎嘎作响？

PDF 301 / UNIT 3007 / verse
SOURCE: O God help me, no!

assimilating like and unlike,

greater! must find a higher, nobler origin.
CHINESE: 上帝啊帮助我，不！

CHINESE: 看那儿！

混合、悠闲地合成人类物质，
将其密封在蒸馏器中并彻底混合，
而工作，伟大的工作，
将秘密地完成。

CHINESE: 它正在发生！

PDF 304 / UNIT 3040 / verse
SOURCE: Remarkable!
(The phial slips out of WAGNERS hands, hovers over FAUST,
and sheds light down upon him.)
The beauty of the place!
Clear streams in a dense grove.
Women, undressing.
Delightful!
But it gets better all the time.
There’s one, I see,
the loveliest of them all,
a splendid creature,
born of a race of heroes,
no, of the gods.
She slips her foot into the transparent waters;
her lovely body’s life flame
cools in the shimmering crystal of the water.
But what is that rush of wings,
that roar and plunge that startles the placid waters?
The women scatter in fright,
and only she, the queen, looks on calmly
with a woman's confident pride.
She beholds with pleasure as the prince of swans,
importunately yet gently,
presses against her knees.
You would almost think he’s at home there.
Suddenly a mist arises,
and with its closely woven veil
conceals that loveliest of scenes.
CHINESE: 了不起！
 （小瓶从瓦格纳斯手中滑落，
悬停在浮士德上方，

茂密的树林中清澈的溪流。
女人，脱衣服。
愉快！
但它一直在变得更好。
我发现，其中有一个是最可爱的，
一个辉煌的生物，诞生于英雄种族，
不，是众神。
她把脚滑进透明的海水里；
她可爱的身体的生命火焰在闪闪发光的水晶水中冷却。
但是，
那惊动平静水面的翅膀的猛冲、轰鸣和猛冲到底是什么？
女人们吓得四散奔逃，
只有她这个女王，平静地看着，
带着女人的自信骄傲。
她高兴地看到天鹅王子固执而温柔地压在她的膝盖上。
你几乎会认为他在那里自在。
突然，一片薄雾升起，
用它密密麻麻的面纱掩盖了那最美丽的景色。

CHINESE: 看看四周！

perhaps even knowledge and integrity.
Farewell!
CHINESE: 收集生活元素并小心翼翼地将它们组合在一起。
考虑“什么”，但更要考虑“如何”。
当我环游世界时，
我可能会发现完成你的“7”的那个点。
那么伟大的目标就会实现。
这样的努力应该得到回报：
黄金、荣誉、名誉、健康长寿，
谁知道呢，甚至可能还有知识和正直。
告别！

PDF 308 / UNIT 3087 / heading
SOURCE: XXXVITI ~~ THE PHARSALIAN PLAIN. DARKNESS
CHINESE: 第37场  法萨卢斯平原 黑夜

PDF 308 / UNIT 3088 / speaker
SOURCE: ERICHTHO
CHINESE: 厄里克托

PDF 308 / UNIT 3089 / verse
SOURCE: How often before have I come to this fearful festival,
I, Erichtho,
Erichtho the Dismal they call me,
but not so repulsive as the spiteful poets
exaggerate me to be with their slanderous tongues,
they who are never done with their praising and carping.
I begin to see stretched out before me in the valley
CHINESE: 我以前多少次来过这个可怕的节日，
我，厄里克托，厄里克托，
他们叫我“忧郁者”，
但并不像那些恶毒的诗人用他们的诽谤性的舌头夸大我那么令人厌恶，
他们永不停歇地赞美和吹毛求疵。
我开始看到山谷中在我面前伸展开来

PDF 309 / UNIT 3090 / verse
SOURCE: the pale gray surge of tents, an afterimage
of that most harrowing and cruel of nights.
How often it has recurred,
and how often to the end of time it will return!
Neither side yields the empire to the other.
Nor will anyone yield it to him
who got it by force and rules it by force.
The man who fails to master his inner self
will lust in his arrogance to master his neighbor's will.
It was here, on this field,
a great battle was fought;
an example of how a mighty power
pits itself against one mightier still,
rips to shreds the richly flowered garland
of Liberty, and coils the stiff laurel
around the conqueror’s head.
Here Pompey dreamed the dream of youthful glories;
there Caesar, waking,
kept watch on fate’s quivering balance.
They'll measure themselves against each other;
and all the world knows who prevailed.
Watch fires burn red,
spreading their glowing largess through the night;
the ground exhales the reflection of long-spilt blood;
and lured there by the night’s miraculous splendor,
the assembling legion of Greek myth gathers.
Hovering uncertainly or sitting at ease
at each fire are the fabulous forms
of ancient days.
The moon, admittedly not full,
but shining brightly, is risen and
sheds its soft radiance everywhere.
The illusion of the tents has disappeared,
and the fires are burning blue.
But what’s that above me?
CHINESE: 浅灰色的帐篷，是那个最痛苦、最残酷的夜晚的残影。
它出现的频率有多少次，
到最后它还会出现多少次！
双方都不会将帝国让给对方。
任何人都不会屈服于以武力获得并以武力统治的人。
一个无法控制自己内在自我的人，
会因傲慢而渴望控制邻人的意志。
正是在这里，在这片战场上，
发生了一场伟大的战斗；
这是一个强大的力量如何与更强大的力量对抗的例子，
如何撕碎自由的花环，
并将僵硬的月桂树盘绕在征服者的头上。
庞培在这里梦想着青春的辉煌；
凯撒醒来，注视着命运颤抖的天平。
他们会互相比较自己；
全世界都知道谁获胜了。
观看火焰燃烧成红色，
在黑夜中散发出炽热的光芒；
地面呼出吐出已久的鲜血的倒影；
希腊神话中的大军被夜晚神奇的光彩所吸引，
聚集在那里。
不确定地盘旋或轻松地坐在每一堆火边，
这是古代神话般的形式。
月亮虽然不是满月，
但光芒四射，升起并向各处散发着柔和的光芒。
帐篷的幻象已经消失，
火焰燃烧着蓝色。
但我上面那是什么？

PDF 310 / UNIT 3091 / verse
SOURCE: A meteor?
Whod have expected it?
Its shine reveals a rounded bodily thing.
I scent life.
I mustn't get too close, being harmful to life.
It would do my name no good and serve no purpose.
It’s descending, I see.
I'll be prudent now and withdraw.
CHINESE: 一颗流星？
谁料到会这样呢？
它的光泽揭示了一个圆形的身体。
我闻到了生命的气息。
不能靠得太近，否则会危及生命。
这对我的名誉没有任何好处，
也没有任何作用。
我看到，它正在下降。
我现在会谨慎行事并退出。

CHINESE: （退出。飞行员从上面出现。）

PDF 311 / UNIT 3117 / verse
SOURCE: Where is she?
But why ask!
If this isn’t the same soil
her foot touched, or the wave that
swirled at her feet, it’s the air, at least,
the air that spoke her speech.
Here, by a miracle, here I am, in Greece!
No sooner I touched the ground,
I knew where I was:
new life surged, warming me after sleep,
like an Antaeus of the spirit.
No matter how strange what I may encounter,
I'll explore this labyrinth of flames in all earnestness.
CHINESE: 她在哪儿？
但为什么要问！
如果这不是她脚踩过的土壤，

奇迹，我在这里，在希腊！
我一落地，就知道自己在哪里：
新的生命涌动，让睡后的我感到温暖，
就像精神的安泰俄斯一样。
无论遇到多么奇怪的事情，
我都会认真地探索这个火焰迷宫。

PDF 312 / UNIT 3119 / heading
SOURCE: XXXVIII ~- ON THE UPPER PENEIOS
CHINESE: 第38场  珀涅俄斯河上游

PDF 312 / UNIT 3120 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

PDF 312 / UNIT 3121 / stage
SOURCE: (Sniffing about.)
CHINESE: （嗅了嗅。）

PDF 312 / UNIT 3122 / verse
SOURCE: I must say,
as I wander among these modest fires,
I feel quite the foreigner. They're mostly
naked as jaybirds, only here and there
do I see a shift. The sphinxes are shameless,
the griffins downright impudent, and all these
winged and curly fleeced things
that display themselves fore and aft!
I have to admit, of course,
that at heart we're as indecent as the next,
but this antique style I find a bit too intense.
It needs mastering, reforming,
in the modern style,
to reflect the new sensibility,
a bit of plaster here and there,
if you know what I mean.
What a repulsive lot!
But I mustn't let it get the better of me.
I'll greet them courteously;
after all, I’m a guest.
Greetings, fair ladies!
And greetings to you, wise old grayfins!
CHINESE: 我必须说，当我在这些不起眼的火堆中漫步时，
我感觉自己完全是个外国人。
他们大部分都像松鸦一样赤身裸体，
只是在某些地方我看到了变化。
斯芬克斯人是无耻的，
狮鹫是彻头彻尾无礼的，
所有这些长着翅膀和卷曲羊毛的东西在船头和船尾展示自己！
当然，我不得不承认，
我们的内心和其他人一样不雅，
但我觉得这种古色古香的风格有点太强烈了。
它需要以现代风格进行掌握、改革，
以反映新的情感，到处都需要一点灰泥，
如果你明白我的意思的话。
多么令人厌恶的一批啊！
但我不能让它战胜我。
我会礼貌地跟他们打招呼；
毕竟我是客人。
问候，美丽的女士们！
向您致意，聪明的老灰鳍鱼！

PDF 321 / UNIT 3240 / verse
SOURCE: Dream? No!
Now, now ’'m awake!
O let them be, those unspeakably lovely figures!
How deeply the dear sight moves me!
Are they dream?
Are they memory?
Once before I felt myself so blest.
Waters idle through cool, dense, swaying thickets,
no rushing sound, no rustle, scarcely a ripple;
and from every side numberless springs flow together
to form this clear, clean, shallow pool for bathing.
And there they play,
young, healthy limbs of lovely women,
seen twice over, to my eye's delight,
in the liquid mirror of the waters.
Some swim boldly,
others wade timidly,
till at last all join in a great white splash
and shrieks and shouts and laughter.
These should content me,
my eyes should feast their fill,
but my senses urge me on, and | turn my gaze
toward that screen of thick foliage, that green veil
that conceals the exalted queen.
What a marvelous sight!
Swans from hidden bays and inlets,
swimming with simple but majestic dignity,
floating silently,
tenderly sociable,
but nonetheless proud and self-assured:
see how each moves his head and beak.
But one of them seems more proud,
more bold than the rest.
Chest thrust forward,

SOURCE: In that sublime assembly of the Argonauts,

a thoughtful, delicate sort,

Comradeship is the only test for courage:
when one man acts he’s praised by all the others.
CHINESE: 在阿尔戈英雄们的盛会中，

当一个人采取行动时，
他会受到所有人的赞扬。

PDF 326 / UNIT 3293 / verse
SOURCE: The telling is easy.
Her brothers the Dioskouroi had
freed their little sister
from the hands of highwaymen.
But they weren't the sort to take a beating lightly,
and after they'd rallied soon were in pursuit.
The brothers and their sister, however,
were slowed down by the swamps near Eleusis.
While her brothers waded,
I paddled my way across.
Once there, she dismounted, and
stroking my dripping mane, thanked me with
sweet compliments.
How gentle she was,
but serious, too, and gracious,
so self-assured, and so young—
an old man’s joy!
CHINESE: 讲述的过程很简单。
她的兄弟迪奥斯库罗伊将他们的小妹妹从强盗手中解救了出来。

在重整旗鼓之后，很快就遭到了追击。
然而，
兄弟俩和他们的妹妹却因埃琉西斯附近的沼泽而减慢了速度。
当她的兄弟涉水时，
我划过河。
一到那里，她就下了马，
抚摸着我滴着水的鬃毛，
用甜蜜的赞美来感谢我。
她是多么温柔，但也很严肃、和蔼，
如此自信，又如此年轻——一个老人的喜悦！

PDF 327 / UNIT 3300 / verse
SOURCE: Then neither will she be bound by time!
Achilles, himself outside of time,
found and made her his own on Pherae.
What rare fortune, to wrest from fate
one’s own true love!
Why, then, shouldn't I, with my fierce longing,
bring back to life this one and only form?
This deathless being,
the equal of gods by her birth,
as great as she is tender,
sublime as she is lovable?
You saw her once long ago,
but I saw her today:
a vision of loveliness, charm and desire.
My mind and soul are possessed, my entire being!
Without her, life is not possible.
CHINESE: 那么她也不会被时间所束缚！
 时间之外的阿喀琉斯在费莱发现了她并将她据为己有。
能从命运中夺取自己的真爱，
是多么难得的幸运啊！
那么，我为什么不应该怀着强烈的渴望，
让这唯一的形式复活呢？
这个不死的存在，与生俱来与神平等，
伟大而温柔，崇高而可爱？
你很久以前见过她一次，

一种可爱、魅力和欲望的景象。
我的思想和灵魂被附身了，
我的整个存在！
没有她，生活就不可能。

SOURCE: My poor man, what an odd case you present!

Nothing grotesque here, only kindness,
concern and gentle good works.
CHINESE: 我可怜的人，你提出的情况多么奇怪啊！

最终阐明医生的思想，

PDF 330 / UNIT 3335 / heading
SOURCE: XL ~~ ON THE UPPER PENEIOS AS BEFORE

SOURCE: Plunge into Peneios! There!

Every pleasure shall revive!
CHINESE: 跳入珀涅俄斯河！

 溅起水花，唱着赞美诗，

一切欢乐都会复苏！

SOURCE: The wildly foaming wave sweeps back,

All earth quakes, the waters shiver,
Bank and gravel smoke and quiver.
Let us flee this, one and all,
Before a worse disaster fall!
Away, and let our joys abound
In bright ocean’s festive round,
Where gentle billows lap the shore,
Rippling, sparkling at Neptune’s door;
where the fair moon floats on high,
shedding light in a glistening sky,
And, mirrored in the ocean’s blue,
Wets us with her sacred dew.
Life is freedom in the ocean,
Here we suffer dread commotion.
CHINESE: 汹涌的泡沫卷回，不再在古老的轨道上；
所有的地震，海水都在颤抖，
河岸和碎石都冒着烟雾和颤抖。
让我们在一场更严重的灾难降临之前，
一并逃离这里！
走吧，让我们的欢乐在明亮的海洋的节日轮回中，
温柔的波浪拍打着海岸，
在涅普顿的门口荡漾，
闪闪发光；
美丽的月亮高高地漂浮着，
在闪闪发光的天空中洒下光芒，
并反射在蓝色的海洋中，
用她神圣的露珠润湿我们。
生命是海洋中的自由，
在这里我们遭受可怕的骚动。

PDF 331 / UNIT 3343 / speaker

PDF 331 / UNIT 3344 / verse
SOURCE: What a tremor, what a rumble,

This way first, then that way shaking!
But you'll not see us budge an inch,
However close the dreadful pinch,
Even though all Hell broke loose!
Now a mound out of the ground
Rises up with a dreadful sound.
We know him well, for who but he
Raised fair Delos from the sea,
Grizzled old codger, from ocean’s floor,
To give mother Leto a shore to bear
Bright Phoebus and his sister there.
Now thrusting, squeezing, straining, prying,
Arms stretched taut and shoulders plying,
Atlas-like he lifts his shoulders,
Heaves up soil and massive boulders,
Sand and gravel, shale and clay,
Where once our tranquil river lay.
Across our peaceful valley floor
He cuts a gap and then no more.
For like a massive caryatid,
Waist-deep in the bare earth squatted,
He stops, unmoving, without a groan,
Bearing a monstrous weight of stone,
Nearer he will not approach,
Nor upon our haunt encroach.
CHINESE: 多么震颤，多么隆隆，
多么可怕的刺耳声，
多么令人震惊，多么颤抖！
先这边晃动，再那边晃动！
但你不会看到我们移动一寸，
 无论可怕的挤压多么接近，
 即使地狱都崩溃了！

 沙子和砾石，页岩和粘土，

然后就再也没有了。
因为就像一个巨大的女像柱，
蹲在齐腰深的光秃秃的土地上，
他停了下来，一动不动，
没有呻吟，承载着巨大的石头重量，
他不会靠近，也不会侵犯我们的出没。

SOURCE: Can't say I’ve learned too much from all this.

CHINESE: 不能说我从这一切中学到了太多。

愚蠢的！

啊，我多么渴望欺骗自己，

They bring their Blocksberg with them!
CHINESE: 我这是到哪儿了？

PDF 342 / UNIT 3440 / verse
SOURCE: Floating around from place to place.

I think they must know the way of
living things, and can show me finally
the wisest course to follow.
CHINESE: 从一个地方漂到另一个地方。

我想他们一定知道生物的生存之道，

PDF 345 / UNIT 3477 / verse
SOURCE: Look there!
That black cloud of cranes!
They threaten to attack the excited mob,

Look at them!
Jabbing down with beak and claw
on the little people! The flash of doom
is heavy in the air. It was criminal
killing those herons at their peaceful pond.
And now that murderous assault
takes a bloody vengeance, pygmy blood
for the brutally shed blood of the herons,
as next of kin rage against their enemies.
What use now are shield and helmet and spear?
What use those heron plumes to the dwarves?
Look, the poor dactyls and ants running for cover!
The army wavers, turns in flight, collapses.
CHINESE: 看那儿！
那黑云般的鹤！

看看他们！
用喙和爪子刺向小人物！
空气中弥漫着厄运的气息。
在宁静的池塘里杀死这些苍鹭是犯罪行为。
现在，这种凶残的袭击带来了血腥的复仇，
侏儒的血是对苍鹭残酷流血的报复，
就像近亲对敌人的愤怒一样。
盾牌、头盔和矛现在有什么用？
这些苍鹭羽毛对矮人有什么用？
看，可怜的指节和蚂蚁正在寻找掩护！
军队动摇，逃跑，崩溃。

PDF 345 / UNIT 3478 / speaker

PDF 345 / UNIT 3480 / verse
SOURCE: Till now I’ve always praised the gods below;
I turn now to those above.
Great goddess on high,
you who are eternal and unaging,
triple-named, triple-formed divinity,
Diana, Luna, Hekate,
I call on you to hear my people's distress!
You who swell the heart with inspiration,
goddess profoundly wise,
placid seeming, fiercely passionate,
open the fearsome chasm of your dark shadows,
and show us without magic your ancient powers!
CHINESE: 至今我一直赞美下面的诸神；
我现在转向上述内容。
伟大的高处女神，
你是永恒不朽的、具有三重名字、三重形态的神性，

我必须说，他因此受到了极大的尊重，
人们来向他表示敬意。
此外，他对很多人都有帮助。

PDF 355 / UNIT 3572 / verse
SOURCE: Troy's day of doom,
cast in epic rhythms,
as terrible now as it was millennia ago.
To that arrogant youth
mine were an old man’s impotent words,
and off he went,
following his bliss,
and Troy fell—
a monster corpse,
stark after long torment,
a welcome feast for Pindus’s eagles.
And Odysseus?
The same story.
I foretold him the wiles of Circe,
the horrors of the Cyclops,
his own hesitancy and his crew’s frivolity,
and I don’t know what all else!
What good did it do him?
Nothing.
Not till much tossed by the seas—
and late enough, too, may I say—
the waves decided to toss him
on a friendly shore.
CHINESE: 特洛伊的末日，以史诗般的节奏铸就，
现在和几千年前一样可怕。
对那个傲慢的年轻人来说，
我的是一个老人无能为力的话语，
他离开了，追随他的幸福，
特洛伊倒下了——一具经过长期折磨后赤裸裸的怪物尸体，
是品都斯山鹰的欢迎盛宴。
奥德修斯呢？
同样的故事。
我向他预言了喀耳刻的诡计、独眼巨人的恐怖、他自己的犹豫和船员的轻浮，
我不知道还有什么！
这对他有什么好处呢？
没有什么。
直到被大海翻来覆去——我可以说，
也已经够晚了——海浪决定把他抛到友好的海岸上。

PDF 356 / UNIT 3577 / verse
SOURCE: Neither Olympus, nor your dry earth

With utter grace

loveliest of all,
she who, since Venus deserted us long ago,
inherits the temple city and the chariot throne,
and is honored now in Paphos as a goddess.
Off with you now!
A father’s joy must not be spoiled
by hate in his heart nor sour words on his lips!
Away to Proteus!
Ask him, that man of miracles,
how one comes to be and
passes from form to form.
CHINESE: 奥林匹斯山和你们干燥的大地都没有孕育出如此娇嫩可爱的生物。

与水元素如此微妙地融为一体，
甚至连泡沫都似乎将它们升起。
 维纳斯的彩虹色贝壳战车将为我们带来最可爱的伽拉忒亚，
自从维纳斯早已抛弃我们后，
她继承了神殿城和战车王座，
现在在帕福斯被尊为女神。
现在就跟你走吧！
父亲的喜悦不应该被心中的仇恨和嘴上的尖酸刻薄所破坏！
前往普罗透斯！
问他，那个创造奇迹的人，
一个人是如何形成并从一个形态转变为另一个形态的。

PDF 375 / UNIT 3772 / verse
SOURCE: O let me kneel,
let me look at her,
let me die,
let me live—
I give myself totally to this
god-sent woman!
As I looked eastward,
awaiting the first rapture of morning,
suddenly, miraculously,
the sun arose in the south!
I saw nothing else,
neither heights nor depths,
neither earth nor sky.
My eyes,
sharp as those of a lynx,
were put to a test now,
as if to fight free
from a deep, dark dream.
I could see nothing!
Battlement, tower, gate!
Nothing!
But then,
oh, then as the swirling mists vanished,
she steps forth,
this goddess we see here!
My gaze, my heart turned toward her,
and I drank in that gentle radiance.
As her beauty now blinds us all,
that same beauty,
blinding me,
made me forget my watchman’s duty,
forget to sound my watchman’s horn.
Threaten me with annihilation—
but beauty silences all wrath.
CHINESE: 哦，让我跪下，让我看着她，

PDF 377 / UNIT 3780 / verse
SOURCE: You see me returned again, my Queen!
A rich man begging another glance.
To see you is to be prince and beggar at once.
What was I then?
What am I now?
What's there to want?
What’s there to do?
What good is the keenest eye
if your radiant throne defeats it?
Out of the East we came,
and so it was all up with the West.
A multitude of nations so vast
the first knew nothing of the last.
When one man fell,
another stood tall,
and a third stood ready with lance in hand,
for each man there were a hundred more.
A thousand might fall,
and no one took notice.
We pushed onward,
onward we stormed,
conquering every place we came to.
Where today I issued lordly commands,
tomorrow another took my place,
plundering, robbing, looking in haste,
eyes peeled, no time to waste,
carrying off women, lovely creatures,
the fittest cattle and every last steed.
I spied out the rarest things.
What others held priceless was worthless to me.
It was treasure I hunted,
and my keen sight led me to it every time.
I saw into pockets,
and my eyes were the key to every chest.
Gold was mine in heaps and mounds,
CHINESE: 你看到我又回来了，
我的女王！
一个有钱人乞求再看一眼。
见到你就等于既是王子又是乞丐。
那时的我是什么？
我现在是什么？
有什么可想要的？
有什么可做的？
如果你的光辉王座打败了它，
那么再敏锐的眼睛又有什么用呢？
我们来自东方，所以西方就完蛋了。
众多的国家如此庞大，
最初的国家对最后的国家一无所知。
当一个人倒下时，另一个人站起来，
第三个人手里拿着长矛准备好，
每人有一百人。
可能会有一千人倒下，
却没有人注意到。
我们向前推进，我们猛烈进攻，
征服了我们所到达的每一个地方。
今天我发出了威严的命令，
明天另一个人取代了我的位置，
掠夺，抢劫，匆忙寻找，
眼睛睁不开，没有时间可以浪费，
抢走了女人，可爱的生物，
最健康的牛和每一匹骏马。
我发现了最稀有的东西。
别人认为无价的东西对我来说毫无价值。
这是我寻找的宝藏，
我敏锐的视力每次都引导我找到它。
我窥探口袋，我的眼睛是打开每个箱子的钥匙。
成堆成堆的黄金是我的，

PDF 378 / UNIT 3781 / verse
SOURCE: and precious jewels.

CHINESE: 和珍贵的珠宝。

SOURCE: His mother calls anxiously to him:

他现在身着花袍，臂上挂着流苏，
胸前飘扬着丝带，就像一个微型阿波罗，
手里拿着金色的七弦琴，
欢快地走近我们头顶的悬崖边缘。
精彩的！
他的父母欣喜地拥抱在一起。
他的头上闪耀着怎样的荣耀？
光环？
很难说。
金色的装饰品？

PDF 386 / UNIT 3844 / verse
SOURCE: Or the radiant glow
of an undaunted spirit’s transcendence?
And so he moves, and every gesture,
as he stands there in his boyhood,
proclaims him the future master of Beauty,
through whose body eternal melodies will flow.
That is how you will hear him,
that is how you will see him,
with a wonder never before known.
CHINESE: 还是无畏精神的超越的光芒？
他就这样移动着，
他童年时站在那里的每一个动作都宣告着他是未来的美之大师，
永恒的旋律将在他的身体中流淌。
这就是你将如何听到他的声音，
这就是你将如何看到他的方式，
带着前所未有的惊奇。

PDF 386 / UNIT 3845 / speaker
SOURCE: CHORUS
CHINESE: 合唱

PDF 386 / UNIT 3846 / verse
SOURCE: Daughter of Crete,
is this what you call a marvel?
Have you never heeded
the instructive words of poets?
Never known IJonia’s ancient tales,
or Hellas’s ancestral legends
of gods and heroes?
Today is a dreary echo of glories past.
Your story pales to the lovely lies
our forebears sang of Hermes,
Maia’s son,
tales more persuasive than truth.
No sooner born,
he’s bound in downy bands,
and then again in sumptuous purple wrappings,
by chattering nurses who know
little what to expect.
Slyly, then, the crafty little rogue works
free his tender, limber limbs,
leaving behind the wrappings,
the purple shell that confined him,
a butterfly slipping from the cell of its chrysalis,
spreading its wings,
flying freely in the radiant sunlight.
CHINESE: 克里特岛的女儿，这就是你所说的奇迹吗？
难道你从来没有留意过诗人的教诲吗？
从未了解过伊乔尼亚的古老故事，
或希腊的众神和英雄的祖先传说？
今天是过去辉煌的沉闷回声。
你的故事与我们的祖先所唱的玛雅之子赫耳墨斯的可爱谎言相形见绌，
故事比事实更有说服力。
他一出生，
就被一群喋喋不休的护士绑在柔软的带子里，
然后又被裹上华丽的紫色包裹，
他们不知道会发生什么。
然后，狡猾的小盗贼狡猾地挣脱了他柔嫩的四肢，
留下了包裹物，留下了限制他的紫色外壳，
一只蝴蝶从蛹的细胞中滑落，
展开翅膀，在灿烂的阳光下自由飞翔。

PDF 387 / UNIT 3847 / verse
SOURCE: Nimblest of the nimble, Hermes then
proves himself the master of artful devices,
the patron god of thieves and rascals
and all self-seekers.
In a flash he’s stolen the sea god’s trident,
the sword from Ares’s scabbard,
Apollo’s bow and arrows,
Hephaestus’s tongs, and would have stolen
Father Zeus’s lightning
were he not in fear of the fire.
He wrestled and tripped up baby Eros,
and while she fondled him
filched the belt from Venus’ waist.
(Music of ravishing pure melody played on strings is heard from
the cavern. All listen, and soon are deeply moved. The music continues
with full orchestra until indicated.)
CHINESE: 赫耳墨斯是敏捷中最敏捷的人，

是小偷、流氓和所有自私者的守护神。
转眼之间，
他偷走了海神的三叉戟、阿瑞斯鞘中的剑、阿波罗的弓箭、赫菲斯托斯的火钳，
如果不是因为害怕火焰，
他甚至会偷走宙斯神父的闪电。
他摔跤并绊倒了小厄洛斯，
当她抚摸他时，他从维纳斯腰间偷走了腰带。
 （洞穴里传出令人陶醉的纯弦乐曲，
所有人都听着，很快就被深深地感动了。
音乐在完整的管弦乐声中继续，
直到指示为止。）

PDF 400 / UNIT 3984 / speaker
SOURCE: A FOURTH PART
CHINESE: 第四部分合唱队

PDF 400 / UNIT 3985 / verse
SOURCE: The rest of you wander wherever you please;
we'll be circling the close-planted hillsides
where the well-staked vines grow green.
There we will see the vintner’s passion
laboring every hour, daily,
at his never-certain goal.
With hoe, with spade, heaping, pruning,
binding, he prays to all the gods,
but to the sun god most of all.
Soft-living Bacchus, caring little about
his faithful servant’s dedication,
rests or lolls in caves or arbors,
having his way with the youngest faun.
All he needs for his half-drunken reveries
is stored around him in skins and flagons,
jars and vessels, right and left of him,
forever on hand, forever preserved,
in cool, shady grottoes for all eternity.
But when all the gods together,
and Helios sun god over all the rest,
have aired and moistened, warmed and roasted,
and piled the cornucopia high,
then, where one sole vintner labored,
there comes a rustling and bustling about,
in every arbor, from vine to vine.
Baskets creak, buckets clatter,
shoulder hampers groan with their loads,
all on their way to the mighty vat,
on to the wine pressers’ lusty dance.
And so the sacred flesh of berries
is rudely crushed, foaming, splashing,
trampled to a repulsive mess.
CHINESE: 其余的人则随心所欲地到处闲逛；
我们将绕着密植的山坡转一圈，
那里的藤蔓长得很茂盛。
在那里，
我们将看到酿酒师的热情每小时、每天都在为他永远不确定的目标而努力。
他用锄头、铁锹、堆放、修剪、捆绑，
向诸神祈祷，但最重要的是向太阳神祈祷。
生活安逸的巴克科斯很少关心他忠实仆人的奉献，
他在山洞或凉亭里休息或懒洋洋地躺着，
与最小的农牧神相处。
他半醉沉思所需的一切都储存在他周围的兽皮、酒壶、罐子和器皿中，
左右两侧，永远在手边，
永远保存在凉爽、阴凉的洞穴里，
直到永远。
但是，当所有的神祇一起，
赫利俄斯太阳神凌驾于其他诸神之上，
已经通风、湿润、温暖和烘烤，
并将聚宝盆堆得高高的时候，
那么，在一个唯一的酿酒师辛勤劳作的地方，
每一个凉亭里，从一棵葡萄树到另一棵葡萄树，
都会传来一阵沙沙声和忙碌声。
篮子吱吱作响，水桶叮当作响，
肩上的​​篮子装载着货物发出呻吟声，
所有这些都在前往巨大的大桶的途中，
在压酒师的激烈舞蹈中。
就这样，
浆果的神圣果肉被粗鲁地压碎、起泡、飞溅、践踏成一团令人厌恶的烂摊子。

PDF 401 / UNIT 3986 / verse
SOURCE: The brazen clash of cymbals now
assaults the ear, and Dionysus,
emerging from his sacred Mysteries,
comes in view with his cleft-footed satyrs,
whirling about their cleft-footed females,
among them Silenus riding his long-eared
beast braying to split the heavens.
Nothing is spared! Nothing! Cleft hooves
trample decency into the dust,
senses whirl, ears are deafened,
drunkards grope their way to wine bowls,
heads are splitting, bellies bursting,
and anyone who calls for caution
only swells the raging tumult,
for to house the brave new wine
wineskins must be emptied fast.
(The curtain falls, PHORKYAS, in the proscenium, rises up a gigantic
figure, then steps down from her cothurni, pushes back her mask and veil,
and reveals herself to be MEPHISTOPHELES—in order, as far as neces-
sary, to comment on the piece in an epilogue.)
CHINESE: 铙钹的猛烈撞击声现在袭击了耳朵，

与他的裂足萨特们一起出现在视野中，

其中西勒诺斯骑着他的长耳野兽，
嘶鸣着撕裂天空。
什么都不能幸免！
没有什么！
蹄子裂开，把体面践踏在尘土里，
感官旋转，耳朵震聋，
醉汉摸索着拿到酒碗，
头颅劈裂，肚子爆裂，
任何人的警告只会加剧汹涌的骚动，
因为容纳勇敢的新酒的酒袋必须很快倒空。
 （幕落落下，
福耳库阿斯在舞台上升起一个巨大的人物，
然后从她的衣帽间走下来，
推开她的面具和面纱，
露出自己是梅菲斯特——以便在必要时在尾声中评论这部作品。）

SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 402 / UNIT 3991 / stage
SOURCE: (Emerging.)

PDF 402 / UNIT 3992 / verse
SOURCE: Leaving behind my chariot of clouds,
I step with caution onto this mountain crest
and see below me the profoundest depths of solitude.
I dismiss it now, that chariot once a robe,
that vehicle that bore me gently
through clear days over land and sea.
Separating from me, it holds its shape,
not scattering, but moves off eastward
as a fleecy cumulus mass while
I look on in wonder.
For now I see it divide, shifting, changing in
wavelike motions, as if seeking another shape.
Yes, and there it has!
There she lies! I see her!
Gigantic! Reclining gloriously!
A woman's form, but like a goddess—
Juno, Leda, Helen!
Majestic and lovely,
it hovers before my sight.
Ah,
but now it begins to dissolve,
disappear, shapeless, broad,
a formless towering mound hanging in the east
like distant icy peaks, mirroring
the deep meaning of those fleeting days.
CHINESE: 我把云车抛在身后，
小心翼翼地踏上山顶，
看到下面是最深的孤独。
我现在不理会它了，
那辆曾经是长袍的战车，
那辆载着我轻轻地穿过陆地和海洋的晴朗日子的车辆。
与我分离后，它保持其形状，
没有散开，而是像绒毛状的积云一样向东移动，
而我则惊奇地看着。
现在我看到它以波浪般的运动分裂、移动、变化，
仿佛在寻找另一种形状。
是的，它就在那里！
她躺在那里！
我看到她了！
巨大！
光荣地躺着！
女子之姿，却如女神——朱诺、勒达、海伦！
雄伟而可爱，盘旋在我的眼前。
啊，但现在它开始溶解、消失，
无形的、广阔的，一个无形的高耸的土丘，
像遥远的冰峰一样悬挂在东方，
反映着那些转瞬即逝的日子的深刻含义。

PDF 403 / UNIT 3993 / verse
SOURCE: But, look, this wisp of cloud,
delicate, shining, lingering still
to cool and cheer my heart and brow—
gently caressing. It rises now,
lightly, higher, higher still,
massing itself together.
What!
Am I deceived?
Seeing things?
This rapturous image
so like my youth’s first, long-lost,
highest good?
A wave of emotions from my earliest days
sweeps through me.
Aurora’s love,
love's early dawn,
the soaring joy, soon felt, scarce understood,
which, if held fast to, would outshine
the most precious things of all!
Now, like the soul’s beauty,
that gracious form grows ever more perfect,
holds,
then rising into the higher regions
takes with itself the best of me.
(A seven-league boot comes clomping in, immediately followed by the
second. MEPHISTOPHELES steps out of them. The boots hurry off.)
CHINESE: 但是，你看，这一缕云，
娇柔的，闪亮的，依然萦绕在我的心眉间，

现在它轻轻地升起，
更高，更高，聚集在一起。
什么！
我被骗了吗？
看东西？
这种欣喜若狂的景象，

奥罗拉的爱，爱的黎明，
飞扬的喜悦，很快就感受到了，
很难理解，如果紧紧抓住，

现在，就像灵魂的美丽一样，
那种优雅的形式变得越来越完美，
然后升入更高的领域，
带走我最好的东西。
 （一只七级联赛的靴子踩了进来，

PDF 404 / UNIT 3999 / stage
SOURCE: (Seriously.)
CHINESE: （说真的。）

PDF 404 / UNIT 4000 / verse
SOURCE: When the Lord God—
and I know why, too—
cast us from the sky to the deepest of pits
(with central fire blazing eternally),
we found ourselves with something
too much illumination,
not to mention overcrowded accommodations.
We devils all began to huff and puff,
fore and aft,
to put out that great blaze,
while roundabout was an acid stink of sulfur
and a pressure of gas so monstrous
it burst through earth’s flat crust,
thick as it was,
with a gigantic bang.
Yes, well, it’s a different story now.
What was once Hell’s bottom is now its top.
It’s on this they base those theories required
to turn things topsy-turvy.
In any case,
we escaped that servile place of confinement
and came to gain free dominion of the air.
An open secret, well preserved,
revealed to the world only later.
CHINESE: 当主上帝——我也知道为什么——把我们从天上扔到最深的坑里（中央的火焰永远燃烧）时，
我们发现自己的照明太多了，
更不用说过度拥挤的住宿了。
我们这些恶魔开始前后呼气，
扑灭那场大火，而周围却弥漫着硫磺的酸臭味，
气体的压力如此巨大，
以至于在巨大的爆炸声中冲破了厚厚的地壳。
是的，好吧，现在情况不同了。
曾经的地狱底部如今已是地狱顶部。
正是基于此，他们建立了扭转局面所需的理论。
无论如何，我们逃离了那个受奴役的禁闭之地，
获得了空中的自由统治权。
这是一个公开的秘密，
保存完好，直到后来才向世人透露。

PDF 405 / UNIT 4005 / verse
SOURCE: That’s what you say!
To you it’s all so clear.
But one who was there knows differently.
And I was there.
I was there when the infernal abyss still seethed and
welled up into rivers shooting flames;
when Moloch, with his hammer,
welded rock to rock,
scattering mountain fragments far and wide.
The land is still littered with great masses of stone.
Who can explain the force that hurled them there?
Philosophers?
How, when they don’t understand?
“Let them lie there,” they say.
“We've made fools enough of ourselves
racking our brains.”
It’s only the common people in their simple dignity
who have understood, and their belief in
ancient wisdom is not to be shaken.
To them it’s a miracle,
and Satan gets his credit!
This is the crutch of faith that lets them hobble
straight to Devil’s Rock and Devil’s Bridge.
CHINESE: 这就是你说的！
对你来说，一切都是那么清楚。

我也在场。
当地狱深渊仍在沸腾并涌入喷出火焰的河流时，
我就在那里；
当莫洛赫用他的锤子将岩石焊接到岩石上时，
山体碎片散落得很远。
这片土地上仍然散布着大量的石头。
谁能解释一下是什么力量把他们扔到那里的？
哲学家？
当他们不明白时怎么办？
 “让他们躺在那里，”
他们说。
 “我们已经绞尽脑汁把自己弄得够傻了。”
只有普通人以朴素的尊严理解，
他们对古老智慧的信仰是不可动摇的。
对他们来说，这是一个奇迹，
撒旦得到了他的功劳！
这是信仰的拐杖，
让他们一瘸一拐地直奔魔鬼岩和魔鬼桥。

PDF 418 / UNIT 4135 / speaker
SOURCE: EMPEROR
CHINESE: 皇帝

PDF 418 / UNIT 4136 / verse
SOURCE: Gentlemen, I welcome this rival emperor,
for I feel now for the first time
that I’m the true one.
I dressed in armor only as a soldier,
but now I wear it with a higher purpose.
At every court festival,
no matter how brilliant,
I always felt a deficiency.
It was danger I lacked.
Danger.
When I tilted, I tilted at the ring,
as you advised, though in my heart
I longed to be truly jousting.
You also counseled against war.
Had I not listened,
by now I would be a hero many times over.
When I saw myself mirrored in that realm of fire,
I felt myself stamped with the seal of independence.
The fearful element pressed horribly in about me,
but it was only illusion—
a grand illusion!
I've always dreamed vaguely of fame and conquest.
I will make up now
for what I so shamefully neglected.
(HERALDS are dispatched to challenge the RIVAL EMPEROR.
Enter FAUST in armor, his visor half closed, along with the THREE
VIOLENT GIANTS, armed and dressed as earlier.)
CHINESE: 先生们，我欢迎这位竞争对手的皇帝，
因为我现在第一次感觉到我是真正的皇帝。
我穿着盔甲只是作为一名士兵，
但现在我穿着它有着更高的目的。
每逢宫廷节日，无论多么辉煌，
我总感到不足。
我缺少的是危险。
危险。
当我倾斜时，我就像你建议的那样倾斜在擂台上，
尽管我内心渴望真正的比武。
您还建议反对战争。
如果我不听的话，现在我已经多次成为英雄了。
当我看到自己在那个火国的镜像中时，
我感觉自己被盖上了独立的印记。
可怕的元素可怕地压迫着我，
但这只是幻觉——一个巨大的幻觉！
我一直模糊地梦想着名誉和征服。
我现在要弥补我如此可耻地忽视的事情。
 （传令官被派去挑战竞争对手的皇帝。
浮士德身穿盔甲，面罩半合着，

PDF 419 / UNIT 4137 / speaker
SOURCE: FAUST
CHINESE: 浮士德

PDF 419 / UNIT 4138 / verse
SOURCE: We come here, Sire,
hoping we're not unwelcome.
Foresight can be a good thing,
even when not needed.
As you know,
the mountain people are well versed
in the lore of nature and rocks.
The spirits who once lived on the low plains
have left there now, more attracted
than ever to mountain ranges.
It’s there they work quietly in cavernous labyrinths,
surrounded by precious gases of metallic vapors,
testing, separating, combining—
with one only purpose:
to discover the not-yet-discovered.
With subtle power of mind
they create transparent forms,
and then in the ever-silent depths of the crystal
they read of events in the upper world.
CHINESE: 陛下，我们来到这里，
希望我们不受欢迎。
即使不需要，远见也可能是一件好事。
如您所知，山民深谙自然和岩石的知识。
曾经居住在低平原上的灵魂现在已经离开了那里，
比以往任何时候都更被山脉所吸引。
在那里，他们在巨大的迷宫中安静地工作，
周围环绕着珍贵的金属蒸气气体，
测试、分离、结合——只有一个目的：
发现尚未发现的东西。
他们用微妙的心灵力量创造出透明的形态，
然后在水晶永远寂静的深处，
他们读到了上层世界发生的事件。

SOURCE: I remind you, Sire, of the

Twigs crackled, kindling,

neither man, God, nor Devil could have saved him;
except for Your Majesty who burst those chains of fire.
CHINESE: 陛下，我让您想起诺尔恰的萨宾死灵法师，
您永远忠诚的仆人。

树枝噼啪作响，着火了，
火焰开始向上蔓延，
干燥的原木交叉排列在周围，
与沥青和硫磺棒混合在一起——无论是人、上帝还是魔鬼都无法拯救他；
除了摧毁那些火链的陛下。

PDF 420 / UNIT 4143 / verse
SOURCE: That was in Rome.
Since then, eternally indebted to you,
he thinks of your welfare alone,
never of himself, forever searching
the stars and the depths of earth for your benefit.
It is his urgent charge sends us here for suppott.
Great forces are at work in the mountains,
Nature works there unimpeded,
though the stupid priestly mind calls it sorcery.
CHINESE: 那是在罗马。
从那时起，他永远感激你，
只考虑你的福祉，而不是他自己，
永远在星星和地球深处寻找你的利益。
是他的紧急任务让我们来这里寻求支持的。
伟大的力量在山中发挥作用，
大自然在那里畅通无阻，
尽管愚蠢的牧师头脑称之为巫术。

PDF 420 / UNIT 4144 / speaker
SOURCE: EMPEROR
CHINESE: 皇帝

PDF 420 / UNIT 4145 / verse
SOURCE: On festive days when we welcome with
open arms pleasure-seeking guests who
crowd and jostle each other in their delight,
we are pleased to see our halls
inadequate to the multitude.
But this worthy man is supremely welcome.
He comes offering support
at the crucial hour of the morning
when our fate hangs in the balance.
And yet I ask you at this most auspicious hour
to withhold your hand from your willing sword,
for at this moment thousands are met
to join in battle for or against me.
A man must depend on himself alone,
he must fight his own battle.
A man who lays claim to crown and scepter
must be ready to prove in single combat
he’s worthy of them.
Let this phantom, this rebel,
who rises up against us,
calling himself emperor,
master of my lands,
the army’s commander,
my vassals’ feudal lord—
let him be dispatched by this my fist
to the Fortress of Death that once held him!
CHINESE: 在节日里，当我们张开双臂欢迎寻欢作乐的客人时，
他们在喜悦中互相拥挤、推挤，
但我们很高兴看到我们的大厅不足以容纳这么多人。
但这个值得尊敬的人是非常受欢迎的。
当我们的命运悬而未决时，
他会在早上的关键时刻前来提供支持。
然而，
我请你在这个最吉祥的时刻把手从你自愿的剑上收回来，
因为此时此刻，
成千上万的人会加入到支持或反对我的战斗中。
一个人必须依靠自己，
他必须自己打仗。
一个声称拥有王冠和权杖的人必须准备好在单场战斗中证明他配得上它们。
让这个幽灵，这个叛逆者，
起来反抗我们，自称皇帝，
我的土地的主人，军队的指挥官，
我的封建领主——让我用我的拳头将他派遣到曾经关押他的死亡堡垒！

PDF 421 / UNIT 4147 / verse
SOURCE: Majesty, to pledge your head,
even in so noble an endeavor,
is ill-advised, that head whose

protecting it and firing your men with courage.
Without that head, what of the limbs?
When the head weakens, they all weaken.
When the head is wounded, all are wounded.
But restore it to health and all are restored.
The arm quickly shields the skull,
the sword parries and returns the blow,
and the victor plants his foot on the slain man’s neck.
CHINESE: 陛下，即使在如此崇高的努力中，
以你的头颅为抵押也是不明智的，
因为头盔上装饰着徽章和羽毛，
保护着你的头颅，并用勇气激励你的部下。
没有了头，四肢又如何呢？
当头部衰弱时，它们都会衰弱。
当头部受伤时，所有人都会受伤。
但恢复健康，一切都恢复了。
手臂迅速护住头骨，
剑格挡并还击，胜利者将脚踩在被杀者的脖子上。

PDF 424 / UNIT 4193 / verse

not to ask where all this came from.

knights and kings and emperors and all,

Well, whatever,
at least it’s no shabby display.
CHINESE: 现在，现在，不要问这一切从何而来。

混乱的疯人院！

PDF 431 / UNIT 4277 / stage
SOURCE: (The ravens have returned.)
CHINESE: （乌鸦回来了。）

PDF 431 / UNIT 4278 / verse
SOURCE: I'll see our exalted Master hears of your industry.
And yet, if you're so minded,
and want to prove masters yourselves,
hurry off to the glowing smithy
where the dwarf folk never tire at hammering
sparks from metal and stone.
Persuade them without fail to give you fire—
brilliant, flashing, sputtering, exploding—
fire such as sparkles in our Master's mind.
Heat lightning from afar,
or the fall of remotest stars,
are common sights on a summer’s night.
But lightning playing in a confusion of shrubbery,
and stars hissing along the moistened earth—
these are sights have power to amaze.
Don’t put yourselves out too much, of course:
ask politely first, and then insist.
CHINESE: 我会看看我们尊贵的师父听说过你们的行业。
然而，如果你有这样的想法，
并且想证明自己是大师，
那就赶紧前往发光的铁匠铺吧，
那里的矮人不知疲倦地用金属和石头锤打火花。
说服他们，
一定要给你火焰——明亮的、闪烁的、溅射的、爆炸的——就像我们主心中闪闪发光的火焰。
来自远处的热闪电，
或者最遥远的星星的坠落，
是夏夜常见的景象。
但闪电在杂乱的灌木丛中划过，
星星在湿润的大地上嘶嘶作响——这些景象都令人惊叹不已。
当然，不要太过分：
先礼貌地询问，然后再坚持。

CHINESE: 浓浓的黑暗笼罩着敌人！

CHINESE: 来自古代金库的空铠甲发现自己在空气中复活了。
它们在那里发出嘎嘎声、撞击声、碰撞声，

PDF 432 / UNIT 4284 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特

PDF 432 / UNIT 4285 / verse
SOURCE: Quite right! They're not to be held back!
Fighting wildly, whack on whack,
The same as in the good old days.
With greaves and armlets, and cuisses, too,
Guelphs and Ghibellines renew
Full tilt their neverending frays.
Firm in inherited rancor, they
put implacable hate on display.
Now far and wide the tumult brays!
And so, at every devil’s féte,
Nothing succeeds like party hate,
Down to the last convulsive throe.
Wild sounds, that scatter fear and panic,
Mingled with piercing yells Satanic,
Ring down into the vale below.
(Tumultuous warlike music in the orchestra, finally giving way
to cheerful military music.)
CHINESE: 完全正确！
他们不应该被阻止！
狂战，打打，一如昔日的美好时光。
圭尔夫派和吉伯林派也带着护胫、臂环和护腿，
重新开始他们永无休止的争斗。
他们怀着与生俱来的仇恨，
将无法平息的仇恨表现出来。
现在喧嚣四起！
因此，在每一次魔鬼的盛宴上，
没有什么比党派仇恨更成功的了，
直到最后的痉挛性阵痛。
狂野的声音，驱散恐惧和惊慌，
 与撒旦般的刺耳叫喊混合在一起，
 响彻下面的山谷。
 （管弦乐队中喧闹的战争音乐，
最后让位于欢快的军乐。）

PDF 436 / UNIT 4342 / speaker
SOURCE: EMPEROR
CHINESE: 皇帝

PDF 436 / UNIT 4343 / verse
SOURCE: Magic or no, it is what it is!
We've won!
The fleeing enemy scattered across the plain.
Look here, the empty throne, the traitor’s treasures.
I now await the arrival of foreign emissaries,
and news that the Empire is again at peace
and loyal to the crown.
There may have been trickery involved, but in the end
we fought our own battle and won it.
Accidents happen,
accidents that work in one’s favor:
a meteor falls from the sky,
blood rains down on the foe,
and from mountain caverns blasts of miraculous sound
that rouse our spirits but utterly confound the enemy.
The loser fell, the object of scorn;
the victor rises, in triumph thanking his God!
But not he alone, a million throats
sing out in one voice:
Te deum laudamus.
But now I'll do what I’ve seldom done.
Look within. A young and lively prince may
squander his days, but time, in the end,
teaches him to value them.
CHINESE: 神奇与否，它就是这样！
我们赢了！
逃跑的敌人散落在平原上。
看这里，空荡荡的王座，
叛徒的宝藏。
我现在等待外国使者的到来，
以及帝国再次和平并忠于王室的消息。
也许其中涉及到诡计，
但最终我们自己打了仗并赢得了胜利。
意外是有的，意外对自己有利：
流星从天而降，血雨洒在敌人身上，
山洞里传出奇声，振奋人心，
却让敌人大吃一惊。
失败者倒下，成为嘲笑的对象；
胜利者站起来，胜利地感谢他的上帝！
但不只是他一个人，
百万张喉咙齐声高唱：
赞美赞美诗。
但现在我会做我很少做的事情。
向内看。
一位年轻活泼的王子可能会浪费自己的时光，
但时间最终会教会他珍惜时光。

PDF 439 / UNIT 4371 / verse
SOURCE: Once the arch entrusts itself to the

In addition,
yours in perpetuity is the exercise
of those feudal privileges which accrue to you
CHINESE: 一旦拱门将自己托付给拱心石，

我的大法官——也是我们的大主教——我刚刚与他们讨论了我的宫廷和宫廷的治理问题。
现在让我们考虑整个帝国，
因为我现在把全部的分量和权威都放在你们五个身上了。
你们将在土地占有方面胜过所有其他人，
因此我从那些支持我们敌人的叛乱分子手中扩大了你们目前的领土。
现在，您不仅拥有辉煌的地产和领地，
而且还有权在机会允许的情况下通过继承、购买和交换的方式进一步扩展它们。
此外，您永久享有行使您所获得的封建特权的权利

PDF 440 / UNIT 4372 / verse
SOURCE: as territorial princes.
The judgments you render are final
and may not be referred to a higher court.
Fees, too, levies, taxes,
rents and tolls, royalties on mines,

all are yours.
This I do to prove to you my gratitude:
you are raised today to a place quite close to Majesty.
CHINESE: 作为领土诸侯。
您做出的判决是最终判决，
不得提交至更高一级法院。
还有费用、征税、税收、租金和通行费、矿山、盐厂和铸币厂的特许权使用费、贡品、安全通行权、封地——全都是你的。
我这样做是为了向您表达我的感激之情：
您今天被提升到一个非常接近陛下的位置。

PDF 440 / UNIT 4376 / verse
SOURCE: I have a yet higher privilege for the five of you.

CHINESE: 我对你们五个人有更高的特权。

PDF 450 / UNIT 4475 / verse
SOURCE: So here we are, back again,

We sailed from port with two ships,
we bring back twenty to scrape at the wharf.

As the saying goes:
Who needs scruples on the open sea?
A fast firm hand is all that counts.
You catch a fish, you catch a ship,
and once you've grappled three,
there’s a fourth and a fifth.
Well, as they say,
you've got the Might you've got the Right.
CHINESE: 所以我们又回来了，
如果主人批准的话，

我们带着两艘船从港口出发，
带了二十艘船回来刮到码头。
如果需要，请用我们的货物来衡量我们的成功。
俗话说：大海上谁还需要顾忌？
快速而坚定的手才是最重要的。
你抓到一条鱼，你抓到一艘船，
一旦你抓到了三条，
就会有第四条和第五条。
好吧，正如他们所说，
你有权力，也有权利。

PDF 452 / UNIT 4491 / verse
SOURCE: Yes, and it’s that ere,

destroy it all,
my worldwide possessions.

at one glance this masterpiece
of the human mind that raises from the sea
new land for humans to live on.

to suffer for the little one doesn’t own.
CHINESE: 是的，就是这个，那个，

PDF 454 / UNIT 4514 / verse
SOURCE: Begotten to see,
To watch day and night,
Sworn to my tower,
The world’s my delight.
I scan the far distance,
I note what is near,
The moon and the stars,
The forests, the deer.
Thus beauty eternal
I see all around,
I’m glad to be praising
The joy I have found.
O fortunate eyes,
Whatever you've seen,
Whatever its outcome,
How lovely it’s been.
CHINESE: 生来观看，昼夜观看，

鹿。
因此，我看到周围都是永恒的美丽，
我很高兴赞美我找到的快乐。
哦，幸运的眼睛，无论你看到了什么，
无论结果如何，它都是多么可爱。

PDF 454 / UNIT 4515 / stage

PDF 454 / UNIT 4516 / verse
SOURCE: It's not alone for enjoyment I’m posted here.
CHINESE: 我发在这里并不是为了享受。

PDF 455 / UNIT 4517 / verse
SOURCE: What horror threatens me
from the world grown dark!
I see flashes of sparks
exploding among the dark lindens;
fire raging, fanned by the whipping breeze;
the moss-covered cottage burning inside!
I see no help in sight, no rescue!
Ah, that dear old couple,
always so careful with fire,
victims now of the smoky blaze.
What a disaster!
The house’s mossy frame stands
black against the crimson flames.
Oh, if only they've escaped,
those dear, good people,
from that raging inferno.
Tongues of flame lick upward
through leaves and boughs,
dry branches catch fire and as they burn
break off and crash to earth.
Why,
why must I have eyes to see this?
Why so farsighted?
There, the chapel,
collapsing under falling branches!
Great swirls of pointed flames rage in the treetops,
and down to their roots the hollow trunks glow crimson.
CHINESE: 黑暗的世界正向我发出怎样的恐怖威胁！
我看到黑暗的椴树丛中迸发出火花；
火势猛烈，微风煽动；
长满青苔的小屋在里面燃烧！
我看不到任何帮助，
没有救援！
啊，那对亲爱的老夫妇，
总是对火如此小心，
现在成了烟雾缭绕的大火的受害者。
真是一场灾难！
房子的长满青苔的框架在深红色的火焰的衬托下显得很黑。
哦，要是他们能逃离那熊熊的地狱就好了，
那些亲爱的好人。
火舌向上舔过树叶和树枝，
干燥的树枝着火，燃烧时折断并坠落到地上。
为什么，为什么我必须有眼睛才能看到这个？
为什么这么有远见？
在那里，教堂在倒下的树枝下倒塌了！
巨大的火焰漩涡在树顶上肆虐，
空心的树干一直延伸到树根，
发出猩红的光芒。

PDF 456 / UNIT 4523 / verse
SOURCE: My watchman grieves,
and my inmost soul is
vexed by this impatient deed.
But no matter.

but a lookout can soon be built,

And I see, too, the old couple
settled in their new surroundings,
that ancient pair who,
grateful for the mercy shown them,
will there live out their final days.
CHINESE: 我的守望者感到悲痛，
我的灵魂深处也因这种不耐烦的行为而烦恼。
但没关系。
椴树林可能会变成惨淡的半烧焦的树干，
但很快就会建起一个瞭望台，
让我可以看到无限的景色。
我也看到，这对老夫妇在他们的新环境中安顿下来，
这对古老的夫妇感谢他们所得到的怜悯，
将在那里度过他们最后的日子。

PDF 456 / UNIT 4524 / speaker
SOURCE: MEPHISTOPHELES AND THE THREE
CHINESE: 梅菲斯特与三个巨人

PDF 456 / UNIT 4525 / stage
SOURCE: (Appear below.)

PDF 456 / UNIT 4526 / verse
SOURCE: Here we are back, sir, on the double.
Excuse us if it didn’t go quite as planned.
We rapped, we knocked at the door,
no one answered.
We rattled it then and started banging,
and the rotted old door caved in.
We yelled at them, threatened,
and got no answer. They didn’t hear,
didn’t want to hear.
But that’s the way these things happen.
We wasted no time and
got them out any way we could.
They didn’t suffer much, the old couple.
They died of shock.
A stranger who'd taken cover there
wanted to fight us, but we soon finished him off.
In the brief course of our wild struggle
coals got scattered and fired some straw.
It’s all a big blaze now,
a funeral pyre for those three.

PDF 459 / UNIT 4571 / verse
SOURCE: Four of them came, three have left.
I made no sense of what they said.
Some echoing word like “breath,”

A dismal word.

Ah,
why have I not won my way to freedom?
Why?
Into the open?
To Nature?
Why?
Not stripped myself of magic,
unlearned all incantations?
Forever!
And stand before you, Nature—
a man—
only a man!
Then, ah, then
it would be worthwhile to be human.
I was that once,
before I plumbed the depths of darkness,
damning the world and myself with evil blasphemies,
the world that is now so thronged with phantoms and
specters that there is no escape.
Daylight may be sweet,
hours of sun and sanity;
and then night comes and trammels us
in a net of dreams.
Home we wend from a happy springtime
romp in the fields,
and a bird croaks.
And what does it croak?
CHINESE: 四个人来了，三个人走了。

为什么？

PDF 461 / UNIT 4592 / verse
SOURCE: I've spent my life chasing through the world,
grabbing at will by the hair all I longed for;
what didn’t please, I let go its way,
what eluded me, I let escape.
My life was desire and satisfaction,
and then again desire—
and with power and might in my grip
I stormed through life;
great and mighty at first,
but now more wisely,
now more prudently.
I know this world well, and I know, too,
that the view into the world beyond is blocked;
and only a fool turns his sheep’s eyes heavenward,
imagining men like himself above the clouds!
Why go floating off into eternity?
Let him stand on firm earth,
let him look around him:
earth has much to offer the man of worth.
What his mind can grasp, that can be his.
When he walks, let him walk with his feet on the ground.
And if spirits haunt him, ignore them,
keep walking, walking, expecting in his path
both pain and pleasure,
never once in all his earthly progress
to know satisfaction.
CHINESE: 我一生都在追寻世界，

那些令我不高兴的，
我就让它顺其自然；
那些让我厌烦的，我就让其逃脱。
我的生活就是欲望和满足，
然后又是欲望——我握着力量和威力，
冲过生活；
起初伟大而强大，但现在更加明智，

让他环顾四周：大地可以为有价值的人提供很多东西。
他的头脑能抓住什么，
那就是他的。

在他的道路上期待着痛苦和快乐，
在他的尘世进步中永远不会有满足感。

PDF 466 / UNIT 4639 / verse

Yes!

wisdom can reach no further: He alone

by conquest every day. And so, ringed round

dedicated to vigorous activity.

PDF 479 / UNIT 4728 / verse
SOURCE: Abyss rests on abyss at my feet;

just as all-powerful Love
shapes and shields all things.
What a roaring tumult about me,
as if all Nature surged like the sea!
And yet it is only the wealth of waters
tumbling in love to the valley below;
the lightning blast has cleared the air
of poison and evil mist:
and all this done in love—
proclaiming the Creative Spirit
that ever wraps us round in glory!
May it light a holy flame
to stir my cold mind,
my dulled senses,
lost in anguish,
bound in chains of
doubt and torment!
God!
Bless me with your light!
CHINESE: 脚下深渊重叠着深渊，

正如无所不能的爱，
塑造并守护一切。

上帝啊！

CHINESE: 蒙福的男童们

PDF 481 / UNIT 4739 / stage
SOURCE: (From inside him.)
CHINESE: （来自他的内心。）

PDF 481 / UNIT 4740 / verse
SOURCE: The sight is great,
but earth’s too gloomy,
it moves us with dread,
we tremble in fear.
Good father,
let us free from here.
CHINESE: 景象固然宏大，
可大地太阴沉，
叫我们心生畏惧，
吓得浑身战栗。
好神父，
快放我们离开这里。

PDF 481 / UNIT 4741 / speaker
SOURCE: PATER SERAPHICUS
CHINESE: Pater Seraphicus（炽爱的神父）

PDF 481 / UNIT 4742 / verse
SOURCE: Mount, rise up, to higher circles,
grow invisibly as God’s
presence makes you ever stronger
with eternal purity.
This it is that feeds the spirit,
this that fills ethereal space,
love’s eternal revelation,
love that leads to pure salvation.
CHINESE: 升起吧，向更高的层天升起，
在无形中渐渐成长；
上帝的临在，以永恒的纯洁，
使你们愈加坚强。
这才是精神的滋养，
这才是充满苍穹的力量，
是爱的永恒启示，
是引人得救的永恒之爱。

SOURCE: ANGELS
CHINESE: 天使们

PDF 481 / UNIT 4747 / stage
SOURCE: (Hovering in the upper atmosphere, bearing FAUST’ immortal part.)
CHINESE: （悬浮在高层大气，承载着浮士德的不朽部分。）

PDF 481 / UNIT 4748 / verse
SOURCE: Saved now, saved from Satan’s power,
this great soul rejoices.
Who strives, and never ceases striving,
is worthy of redemption.
And if the love that reigns on high
has shown him gracious favor,
then angel hosts will sing his praise,
and give him joyous welcome.
CHINESE: 得救了，脱离了撒但的权势，
这伟大的灵魂欢欣鼓舞。
凡努力追求、永不停止追求的人，
都值得获得救赎。
倘若高天上主宰一切的爱，
已把恩宠赐给了他，
那么天使的军旅便将颂扬他，
满怀喜悦，欢迎他的到来。

PDF 482 / UNIT 4749 / speaker
SOURCE: THE YOUNGER ANGELS
CHINESE: 年轻的天使们

PDF 482 / UNIT 4750 / verse
SOURCE: Roses from the hands of women,
love-devoted penitents,
rosebuds of their love to scatter,
helped us gain this radiant gift,
brought our Great Work to fulfillment,
brought to us this noble prize.
Devils, evil, fled before us,
struck not with the pains of Hell,
but love's searing fire from Heaven,
accosted by the pangs of love.
Even Satan, Hell’s great master,
felt the ravishing stings of love.
Praise the hallowed!
Hell is harrowed!
CHINESE: 玫瑰来自那些女子的手，
来自以爱奉献的忏悔者；
散落她们爱的花蕾，
帮助我们赢得这光辉的赐予，
使我们的伟业得以完成，
使这高贵的珍宝归于我们。
邪恶的魔鬼在我们面前逃散，
刺痛他们的并非地狱之苦，
而是天上炽烈的爱火，
是爱的痛楚向他们袭来。
就连地狱的主宰撒但，
也尝到了爱的醉人刺痛。
赞美这神圣的一刻！
地狱的权势已被攻破！

PDF 482 / UNIT 4751 / speaker
SOURCE: THE MORE PERFECT ANGELS
CHINESE: 更臻完善的天使们

PDF 482 / UNIT 4752 / verse
SOURCE: To bear on high
this remnant of earth
is no small task.
Burned to ashes
it would not be pure.
When power of spirit
unites elementally,
no angel force can
wrest them asunder,
the two from the one.
Love, only love
eternal can part them.
CHINESE: 把这尘世的余留，
携往高天，
可不是一件轻易的事。
即使烧成灰烬，
它也未必纯净。
当精神的力量，
与物质从根本上融为一体，
没有天使的力量，
能把合一的二者，
重新分开。
唯有爱，唯有永恒的爱，
才有力量使它们分离。

PDF 483 / UNIT 4761 / stage

PDF 483 / UNIT 4762 / verse
SOURCE: Supreme Mistress of sky,
Mother of earth below,
in your bright azure vaulted,
CHINESE: 天空的最高女主人，
下方的大地之母，在你明亮的蔚蓝拱顶中，

PDF 484 / UNIT 4763 / verse
SOURCE: show me, show, oh, show,
your mystery exalted!
Bless all that in man’s heart
has fired emotions,
gentler, dearer,
and, with sacred love inspired,
draws it to you,
ever nearer!
Since your wish inspires our will,
what could daunt or curb it?
Bid us only to be still,
nothing could disturb it.
Virgin, pure from spot or taint,
Mother, holy, tender,
Queen, elect of us, and saint,
throned with God in splendor!
Light clouds entwine
about her face—
penitent women,
hovering in space,
kneeling to render
lowly contrition,
entreating remission
of sins from her grace.
Spotless maiden,
undisturbed
in your matchless glory,
these penitent women,
led astray,
may bring to you
their story.
Those whom frailty has bound
are hard to save, or never;
what soul can break the bonds of sin
by only his endeavor?
CHINESE: 向我显现，啊，向我显现，
你崇高的奥秘！
请祝福人心中一切，
曾被唤起的情感，
使它们更温柔、更亲爱，
由神圣的爱鼓舞，
向你靠拢，
愈来愈近！
既然你的愿望激励我们的意志，
还有什么能使它畏缩、将它阻止？
只消你命我们安静，
便没有什么能再将它扰动。
无瑕无垢的贞女，
圣洁温柔的母亲，
我们所敬奉的女王与圣者，
与上帝同坐荣光的宝座！
轻云盘绕，
环抱她的面容——
忏悔的妇女们，
正悬浮在空中，
俯身跪下，
谦卑地痛悔，
祈求她的恩典，
赦免她们的罪。
纯洁无瑕的少女，
你无可比拟的荣光，
永不会受到侵扰；
这些迷失过的忏悔女子，
可以向你，
诉说她们的遭遇。
那些被软弱捆缚的人，
极难得救，甚至无从得救；
有哪个灵魂，仅凭自己的努力，
就能挣断罪恶的锁链？

CHINESE: 凭我那份爱，

全不顾法利赛人的轻蔑；

我满怀敬意，
如此丰厚地倾倒；
凭我披散的头发，
曾环绕他神圣的肢体，
将它们轻轻擦干——
